\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}X(t;t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}x=0
,
\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}V(t;t,x,v)=\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}v=0
. Then we obtain

(2.48)

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}X(s;t,x,v)|&\lesssim\int^{t}_{s}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}V(s_{1};t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}s_{1}\\
&\lesssim\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{s_{1}}|\nabla_{x}^{3}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})(X(s_{2};t,x,v))||\nabla_{v}X(s;t,x,v)||\nabla_{x}X(s;t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}s_{2}\mathrm{d}s_{1}\\
&\qquad+\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{s_{1}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}X(s_{2};t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}s_{2}\mathrm{d}s_{1}.\end{split}

Using Fubini’s theorem, we derive

\begin{split}\eqref{eq1:X_xv_Lp}\lesssim&\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{s_{1}}|\nabla_{x}^{3}\phi_{f}(X(s_{2};t,x,v))||\nabla_{v}X(s;t,x,v)||\nabla_{x}X(s;t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}s_{2}\mathrm{d}s_{1}\\
&+\int^{t}_{s}(s_{2}-s)\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}X(s_{2};t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}s_{2}.\end{split}

This, together with (
2.3
) and the Gronwall’s inequality, implies that

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}X(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim|t-s|e^{\frac{3}{2}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(t-s)^{2}}\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{s_{1}}|\nabla_{x}^{3}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})(X(s_{2};t,x,v))|\mathrm{d}s_{2}\mathrm{d}s_{1}.\end{split}

The remaining estimate follows analogously to the first part, and we obtain (
2.45
).
∎

Lemma 8
.

For
2<p<3
, the following estimates for
\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}
hold:

(1)

For the stationary problem (
1.7
),

\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(2)

For the dynamical problem (
1.17
),

\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}(h+f)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w(h+f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Proof.

For the stationary problem (
1.7
), applying the elliptic estimate to the equation for
\phi_{h}
in (
1.7
) and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}&\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}+\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{v}}\|\sqrt{\mu}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{v}}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

We omit the dynamical problem (
1.17
) case, as it is similar to the stationary case.
∎

Lemma 9
.

For
p>3
, the following
C^{1}
estimates hold:

(1)

For the stationary problem (
1.7
),

(2.49)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}\|_{C^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(2.50)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}\|_{C^{1}_{x}}\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(2)

For the dynamical problem (
1.17
),

(2.51)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(2.52)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Proof.

For the stationary problem (
1.7
), using Morrey’s inequality and the assumption
p>3
, we have

\|\phi_{h}\|_{C^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\|\phi_{h}\|_{W^{2,p}_{x}}\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Furthermore, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality imply that

\|\phi_{h}\|_{C^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|h\|_{L^{p}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p-2}{p}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{\frac{2}{p}}\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

We omit the dynamical problem (
1.17
) case, as it is similar to the stationary case.
∎

2.2.
Properties of the collision operators
\mathcal{L}
and
\Gamma

In this section, we introduce the properties of the linearized collision operator
\mathcal{L}
and the nonlinear collision operator
\Gamma
defined in (
1.8
). It is known (see
[
17
]
) that the linearized operator
\mathcal{L}
admits the decomposition

\mathcal{L}f=\nu(v)f-Kf,

where
\nu(v)
denotes the collision frequency and
K
denotes the integral operator.

Lemma 10

(
[
17
]
)
.

The integral operator
K
is a compact operator on
L^{2}_{v}
, and is defined by

Kf(x,v)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)f(x,u)\,\mathrm{d}u.

Define
\mathbf{k}_{\varrho}(v,u):=e^{-\varrho|v-u|^{2}}/|v-u|
for some
\varrho>0
. Then the kernel
\mathbf{k}(v,u)
satisfies

|\mathbf{k}(v,u)|\lesssim\mathbf{k}_{\varrho}(v,u).

Moreover, the collision frequency
\nu(v)
satisfies

\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}\big(|v|+1\big)\text{ with }\nu_{0}>0,\qquad|\nabla_{v}\nu(v)|\lesssim 1.

Lemma 11

(
[
5
,
23
]
)
.

Let
\mathbf{k}(v,u)
be the kernel of the integral operator
K
introduced in Lemma
10
. Recall the definition of
\mathbf{k}_{\varrho}(v,u)
in Lemma
10
. Then the following estimates hold.

(1)

Let
0\leq\theta<\frac{1}{4}
. There exists
\tilde{\varrho}>0
such that

\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\lesssim\mathbf{k}_{\tilde{\varrho}}(v,u)=e^{-\tilde{\varrho}|v-u|^{2}}/|v-u|.

Moreover,

(2.53)

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|},

and for
1<p<2
, we have

(2.54)

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}^{p}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|}.

(2)

For
N\gg 1
, we have

(2.55)

\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\mathbf{1}_{|v-u|>\frac{1}{N}}\leq C_{N},

and

\int_{|u|>N\text{ or }|v-u|\leq\frac{1}{N}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\frac{1}{N}\leq o(1).

(3)

For some
\varrho>0
, the derivative of
\mathbf{k}(v,u)
satisfies

|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{k}(v,u)|\lesssim\frac{\langle v\rangle}{|v-u|}\mathbf{k}_{\varrho}(v,u),\qquad|\nabla_{u}\mathbf{k}(v,u)|\lesssim\frac{\langle u\rangle}{|v-u|}\mathbf{k}_{\varrho}(v,u).

Moreover, there exists
\tilde{\varrho}>0
such that

|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{k}(v,u)|\frac{e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\lesssim\frac{[1+|v|^{2}]}{|v-u|}\mathbf{k}_{\tilde{\varrho}}(v,u),\qquad|\nabla_{u}\mathbf{k}(v,u)|\frac{e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\lesssim\frac{[1+|u|^{2}]}{|v-u|}\mathbf{k}_{\tilde{\varrho}}(v,u).

Proof.

We only prove (
2.54
), while the other estimates can be found in
[
5
,
23
]
.
It follows from
[
17
]
that

\displaystyle\mathbf{k}^{p}(v,u)\leq C\big(|v-u|^{p}+|v-u|^{-p}\big)e^{-\frac{p}{8}|v-u|^{2}-\frac{p}{8}\frac{(|v|^{2}-|u|^{2})^{2}}{|v-u|^{2}}},

which contains the singular term
|v-u|^{-p}
.
Following the proof of Lemma 3 in
[
23
]
, we set
\eta=v-u
and decompose

\eta_{\parallel}=\Big(\eta\cdot\frac{v}{|v|}\Big)\frac{v}{|v|},\qquad\eta_{\perp}=\eta-\eta_{\parallel}.

Then we compute that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}^{p}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)e^{\theta|v|^{2}}}{\nu^{n}(u)e^{\theta|u|^{2}}}\,\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{|\eta_{\perp}|^{p}}+1\Big)e^{-\frac{C}{4}|\eta|^{2}}\Big\{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-C|v||\eta_{\parallel}|}\,\mathrm{d}|\eta_{\parallel}|\Big\}\mathrm{d}\eta_{\perp}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{|\eta_{\perp}|^{p}}+1\Big)e^{-\frac{C}{4}|\eta_{\perp}|^{2}}\Big\{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-C|y|}\,\mathrm{d}y\Big\}\mathrm{d}\eta_{\perp}\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|},

where we use
p<2
in the second line to ensure local integrability with respect to
\eta_{\perp}
.
∎

Lemma 12

(
[
17
]
)
.

For the nonlinear collision operator
\Gamma
in (
1.8
), the following estimates hold.

(1)

We have the following
L^{2}
and
L^{\infty}
estimates:

\begin{split}&\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2},\\
&\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(f,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}.\end{split}

(2)

The
v
derivative on
\Gamma(f,f)
is computed as

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\Gamma(f,f)=\Gamma(\partial_{v}f,f)+\Gamma(f,\partial_{v}f)+\Gamma_{v}(f,f),

where
\Gamma_{v}(f,f)
is given via the Carleman representation:

\begin{split}\Gamma_{v}(f,f)&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|u\cdot\omega|f(v+u_{\perp})f(v+u_{\parallel})\partial_{v}\sqrt{\mu(v+u)}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u\\
&\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|u\cdot\omega|f(v+u)f(v)\partial_{v}\sqrt{\mu(v+u)}\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u.\end{split}

Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma_{v}(f,f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

(3)

We define
\Gamma_{\text{loss}}
and
\Gamma_{\text{gain}}
as follows:

\begin{split}\Gamma_{\text{loss}}(f,g)&:=\frac{Q_{\text{loss}}(\sqrt{\mu}f,\sqrt{\mu}g)}{\sqrt{\mu}}\ \text{ with }\ Q_{\text{loss}}(F,G):=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(v-u)\cdot\omega|F(u)G(v)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u,\\
\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(f,g)&:=\frac{Q_{\text{gain}}(\sqrt{\mu}f,\sqrt{\mu}g)}{\sqrt{\mu}}\ \text{ with }\ Q_{\text{gain}}(F,G):=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(v-u)\cdot\omega|F(u^{\prime})G(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u.\end{split}

There exists a kernel
\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)
such that

(2.56)

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{\text{loss}}(f,\partial_{x,v}f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\nu(v)|\partial_{x,v}f(v)|,

(2.57)

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(f,\partial_{x,v}f)+\Gamma(\partial_{x,v}f,f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)|\partial_{x,v}f(u)|\mathrm{d}u.

Moreover, for
0\leq\tilde{\theta}\ll\theta<\frac{1}{4}
, the kernel
\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)
satisfies

\begin{split}\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)w_{\tilde{\theta}(v)}}{\nu^{n}(u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}&\lesssim e^{-\tilde{\varrho}|v-u|^{2}}/|v-u|\ \text{ for some }\tilde{\varrho}>0,\\
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\mathrm{d}u&\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|},\end{split}

and for
1<p<2
, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}^{p}_{1}(v,u)\frac{\nu^{n}(v)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{\nu^{n}(u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|v|}.

We remark that these bounds are of the same type as those satisfied by
\mathbf{k}(v,u)
in Lemma
11
.

Proof.

We only prove (
2.56
) and (
2.57
), while the other estimates can be found in
[
17
]
or proved similarly to Lemma
11
.
For (
2.56
), we have

\begin{split}\Gamma_{\text{loss}}(f,\partial_{x,v}f)&=\partial_{x,v}f(v)\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(v-u)\cdot\omega|\sqrt{\mu(u)}f(u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}\omega\\
&\leq\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}|\partial_{x,v}f(v)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-u|\sqrt{\mu(u)}w^{-1}(u)\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}|\partial_{x,v}f(v)|\nu(v).\end{split}

For (
2.57
), we have

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(f,\partial_{x,v}f)+\Gamma(\partial_{x,v}f,f)|\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\frac{Q_{\text{gain}}(w^{-1}\sqrt{\mu},\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x,v}f)+Q(\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x,v}f,w^{-1}\sqrt{\mu})}{\sqrt{\mu}}.

By direct computation, the kernel
\mathbf{k}(v,u)
in Lemma
11
satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{Q_{\text{gain}}(\mu,\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x,v}f)+Q(\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x,v}f,\mu)}{\sqrt{\mu}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)\partial_{x,v}f(u)\mathrm{d}u.

Following the argument in
[
17
]
, there exists another kernel
\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)
such that

|\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(f,\partial_{x,v}f)+\Gamma(\partial_{x,v}f,f)|\lesssim\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}_{1}(v,u)|\partial_{x,v}f(u)|\mathrm{d}u.

Therefore, we conclude (
2.57
).
∎

2.3.
Property of the kinetic weight

In this section, we study the properties of the kinetic weights
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
in (
1.12
) for the stationary problem and
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)
in (
1.18
) for the dynamical problem.

Throughout this section, we assume that the boundary condition for the external field
\phi_{E}
in both (
1.7
) and (
1.17
), and the self-consistent fields satisfy

\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}<\frac{1}{20}C_{E},\qquad\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}<\frac{1}{20}C_{E}.

All lemmas in this section apply to both kinetic weights
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
and
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)
2
2

2

In later sections, we will specify whether we are considering
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
in (
1.12
) or
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)
in (
1.18
).
. For simplicity, we slightly abuse notation (except in Lemma
18
and Lemma
19
, where the stationary and dynamical settings are treated separately) and write

\begin{split}E(t,x)&:=\begin{cases}-\nabla_{x}\big(\phi_{h}(x)+\phi_{E}(x)\big),&\text{ for stationary problem},\\[5.0pt]
-\nabla_{x}\big(\phi_{f}(t,x)+\phi_{E}(x)\big),&\text{ for dynamical problem},\end{cases}\\[5.0pt]
\alpha(t,x,v)&:=\begin{cases}\alpha_{h}(x,v),&\text{ for stationary problem},\\[5.0pt]
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v),&\text{ for dynamical problem}.\end{cases}\end{split}

We remark that for the stationary problem, both
\alpha(t,x,v)=\alpha_{h}(x,v)
and
E(t,x)=-\nabla_{x}\big(\phi_{h}(x)+\phi_{E}(x)\big)
are independent of time.

Recall from Lemma
1
that for any
x\in\Omega_{\delta}
with
0<\delta\ll 1
, there exists a unique
\tilde{x}\in\partial\Omega
satisfying that
dist(x,\tilde{x})=dist(x,\partial\Omega)
.
Then the above assumptions imply that

(2.58)

E(t,x)\cdot n(\tilde{x})>\frac{C_{E}}{2}\ \text{ for }x\in\Omega_{\delta}.

Furthermore, from (
1.12
) and (
1.18
), and the choice
\delta^{\prime}\ll\delta^{1/2}
, we have

\displaystyle\big[2(E(t,\tilde{x})\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi(\tilde{x}))\xi(x)\big]^{1/2}\gtrsim C_{E}^{1/2}\delta^{1/2}>\delta^{\prime}\ \text{ for }(1-\varepsilon)\delta\leq\mathrm{dist}(x,\partial\Omega)\leq\delta.

Hence,
\alpha(t,x,v)=\delta^{\prime}
when
(1-\varepsilon)\delta\leq\mathrm{dist}(x,\partial\Omega)\leq\delta
. Therefore, the kinetic weight
\alpha(t,x,v)
is continuous across the interface
\{x\in\Omega\mid\mathrm{dist}(x,\partial\Omega)=\delta\}\subset\partial\Omega_{\delta}
.

We start with the velocity lemma for the kinetic weight as follows.

Lemma 13

(Velocity Lemma)
.

There exists a constant
C_{0}=C_{0}(C_{E},\Omega)>0
such that for any
t-t_{b}\leq s\leq t
,

e^{-C_{0}}\alpha(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))\leq\alpha(t,x,v)\leq e^{C_{0}}\alpha(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))

Proof.

For simplicity, we write
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
throughout the proof.
From Lemma
5
, there exist
0<\delta\ll 1
and
s\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq t
such that

X(t^{\prime})\in\begin{cases}\Omega\setminus\Omega_{\delta},&t^{\prime}\in[t_{1},t_{2}],\\[5.0pt]
\Omega_{\delta},&t^{\prime}\in[s,t_{1})\cup(t_{2},t].\end{cases}

If
X(t^{\prime})\in\Omega_{\delta}
for all
s\leq t^{\prime}\leq t
, we set
t_{1}=t_{2}
. Moreover, by (
2.35
) in Lemma
5
, there exists a constant
C_{\phi_{E},\delta}>0
such that

(2.59)

|t_{1}-s|+|t-t_{2}|\leq C_{\phi_{E},\delta}.

Then we compute

\frac{\alpha(X(s),V(s))}{\alpha(t,x,v)}=\frac{\alpha(X(s),V(s))}{\alpha(X(t_{1}),V(t_{1}))}\frac{\alpha(X(t_{1}),V(t_{1}))}{\alpha(X(t_{2}),V(t_{2}))}\frac{\alpha(X(t_{2}),V(t_{2}))}{\alpha(t,x,v)}=\frac{\alpha(X(s),V(s))}{\alpha(X(t_{1}),V(t_{1}))}\frac{\alpha(X(t_{2}),V(t_{2}))}{\alpha(t,x,v)},

where we used that
\alpha(X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime}))=\delta^{\prime}
for
t_{1}\leq t^{\prime}\leq t_{2}
.
Using Lemma 7 in
[
2
]
, we have

\displaystyle e^{-C_{\phi_{E},\delta}(|t_{1}-s|+|t-t_{2}|)}\leq\frac{\alpha(X(s),V(s))}{\alpha(X(t_{1}),V(t_{1}))}\frac{\alpha(X(t_{2}),V(t_{2}))}{\alpha(t,x,v)}\leq e^{C_{\phi_{E},\delta}(|t_{1}-s|+|t-t_{2}|)}.

This, together with (
2.59
), concludes the lemma.
∎

Corollary 4
.

Let
0<\delta\ll 1
be the constant in Lemma
5
. Suppose that

X(s;t,x,v)\in\Omega_{\delta}
for all
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\leq s\leq t
. Then we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{v}X(s;t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(t,x,v).

Proof.

We apply (
2.3
) in Lemma
2
, Corollary
3
, and Lemma
13
to obtain

|\nabla_{v}X(s)|\lesssim|t-s|\lesssim t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)\lesssim\min\{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|,\ 1\}\lesssim\alpha(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\lesssim\alpha(t,x,v).

∎

Using the velocity lemma, the following lemma shows that the kinetic weight
\alpha(t,x,v)
compensates for the singularity term
|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|^{-1}
arising in the characteristics from Lemma
3
.

Lemma 14
.

Assume that
t_{b}<\infty
. Then

\alpha(t,x,v)\lesssim|n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)|.

Consequently,

\frac{1}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)|}\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)}.

Proof.

From Lemma
13
, we have

\alpha(t,x,v)\lesssim\alpha(t-t_{b},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}}).

Using the definition of
\alpha(x,v)
in (
1.12
) and (
1.18
), and the fact that
\xi(x_{\mathbf{b}})=0
, we obtain

\alpha(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\lesssim\min\{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|,\ \delta^{\prime}\}\lesssim|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|,

and thus conclude the lemma.
∎

Lemma 15
.

Let
\delta^{\prime}>0
be the constant defined in (
1.12
). For any
c>0
and
2<p<3
, we have

\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<\infty}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/2}e^{-c|v_{\mathbf{b}}|^{2}}}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|^{p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(\delta^{\prime})^{-p+2-\varepsilon}\lesssim 1,

where
0<\varepsilon\ll 1
.

Proof.

To prove the lemma, we use the following change of variables:

(2.60)

\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<\infty}|f(t,x,v)|\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v=\int_{\gamma_{-}}\int_{0}^{t_{\mathbf{f}}}|f(t,X(t+s;t,x,v),V(t+s;t,x,v))||n(x)\cdot v|\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}.

Here
0<s<t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
, and thus
X(t+s;t,x,v)
represents a forward characteristic. We refer to
[
3
]
for the proof of this change of variables.

Note that for any
(x,v)\in\gamma_{-}
and
0<s<t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
,

\begin{split}t_{\mathbf{b}}(t+s,X(t+s;t,x,v),V(t+s;t,x,v))&=s,\\
x_{\mathbf{b}}(t+s,X(t+s;t,x,v),V(t+s;t,x,v))&=x,\\
v_{\mathbf{b}}(t+s,X(t+s;t,x,v),V(t+s;t,x,v))&=v.\end{split}

This, together with the change of variables (
2.60
), implies that

(2.61)

\begin{split}&\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<\infty}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/2}e^{-c|v_{\mathbf{b}}|^{2}}}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|^{p}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&=\int_{\gamma_{-}}\int_{0}^{t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}s/2}e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|n(x)\cdot v|^{p}}|n(x)\cdot v|\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}=\int_{\gamma_{-}}\int_{0}^{t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}s/2}e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|n(x)\cdot v|^{p-1}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}.\end{split}

For the characteristic
X(t+s;t,x,v)
with
0<s<t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
, there exists
0<t_{*}\leq t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
such that

X(t+s;t,x,v)\in\begin{cases}\Omega_{\delta},&s\in[0,t_{*}),\\[5.0pt]
\partial\Omega_{\delta}\setminus\partial\Omega,&s=t_{*}.\end{cases}

If
t_{*}=t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
, then (
2.34
) in Lemma
5
implies
t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)\lesssim|n(x)\cdot v|
.
Otherwise,
t_{*}<t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)
. From (
1.12
) and Lemma
13
, we obtain

|n(x)\cdot v|\gtrsim\alpha(t,x,v)\gtrsim\alpha(t+t_{*},X(t+t_{*};t,x,v),V(t+t_{*};t,x,v))=\delta^{\prime}.

Combining the two cases and using the assumption
2<p<3
, we derive

\begin{split}&\int_{\gamma_{-}}\int^{t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)}_{0}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}s/2}e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|n(x)\cdot v|^{p-1}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}\\
&\lesssim\int_{\gamma_{-}}\frac{e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|n(x)\cdot v|^{p-2}}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}+\int_{\gamma_{-}}\int_{0}^{t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)}\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}s/2}e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|n(x)\cdot v|^{1-\varepsilon}(\delta^{\prime})^{p-2+\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}\lesssim\frac{1}{(\delta^{\prime})^{p-2+\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

This, together with (
2.61
), completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 16
.

For any
c>0
and
q<1
, we have

(2.62)

\sup_{x\in\Omega}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{\alpha^{q}(t,x,v)}\,\mathrm{d}v\lesssim 1.

Proof.

If
x\in\Omega_{\delta}\setminus\Omega
, then from the definitions in (
1.12
) and (
1.18
), we have
\alpha^{q}(t,x,v)=\delta^{\prime}
, and thus (
2.62
) holds.
Otherwise,
x\in\Omega_{\delta}
. From the definition of
\alpha
, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{\alpha^{q}(t,x,v)}\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{|v\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi|^{q}}\mathrm{d}v\ \text{ with }\ |\nabla_{x}\xi|\neq 0.

Let
\big(\frac{\nabla_{x}\xi}{|\nabla_{x}\xi|},\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\big)
be an orthonormal basis of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. We decompose

v=v_{n}\frac{\nabla_{x}\xi}{|\nabla_{x}\xi|}+v_{\tau_{1}}\tau_{1}+v_{\tau_{2}}\tau_{2}\ \text{ with }\ v_{n}=v\cdot\frac{\nabla_{x}\xi}{|\nabla_{x}\xi|},\ v_{\tau_{1}}=v\cdot\tau_{1},\ v_{\tau_{2}}=v\cdot\tau_{2}.

Then we derive that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|v|^{2}}}{\alpha^{q}(t,x,v)}\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-c\big(v_{\tau_{1}}^{2}+v_{\tau_{2}}^{2}\big)}\mathrm{d}v_{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{e^{-c|v_{n}|^{2}}}{|v_{n}|^{q}}\mathrm{d}v_{n}\lesssim 1.

Combining the two cases, we conclude the proof of the lemma.
∎

The following lemma includes two nonlocal-to-local estimates from
[
2
]
and one new estimate proved here.

Lemma 17

(Lemma 11 in
[
2
]
)
.

Recall the collision frequency
\nu(v)
in Lemma
10
. Let
\tilde{\nu}(x,v)
satisfy

\tilde{\nu}(x,v)>\frac{1}{8}\nu(v).

For any
c>0
and
0<\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have

\begin{split}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s&\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)},\\
\int^{t}_{t-\varepsilon}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-c|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{\alpha(t,x,v)}+\varepsilon.\end{split}

Moreover, for some sufficiently large
N\gg 1
, we have

\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}}\frac{e^{-c|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\alpha(t,x,v)}.

Proof.

We only prove the last estimate, while the other estimates can be found in
[
2
]
.
Using Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
, we have

(2.63)

\begin{split}&\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|<\frac{1}{N}}\frac{e^{-C|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\\
&\lesssim\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-C|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\times\Big(\int_{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|<\frac{1}{N}}\frac{e^{-C|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\mathrm{d}u\Big)^{1/2}\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

Since
N\gg 1
and
\tilde{\nu}(x,v)>\frac{1}{8}\nu(v)>\frac{\nu_{0}}{8}
, we apply Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}s
to obtain

(
2.63
)

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\Big(\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{e^{-C|V(s;t,x,v)-u|^{2}}}{|V(s;t,x,v)-u|}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}(s,X(s;t,x,v),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\frac{1}{\alpha^{1/2}(t,x,v)}\lesssim o(1)\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)},

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
\alpha(t,x,v)\leq\delta^{\prime}\ll 1
.
∎

Lemma 18
.

For
2<p<3<q
, the following estimates for
\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{f}\|_{C^{0,1-\frac{3}{q}}_{x}}
hold:

(1)

For the stationary problem (
1.7
),

\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{h}\|_{C^{0,1-\frac{3}{q}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(2)

For the dynamical problem (
1.17
),

\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{f}\|_{C^{0,1-\frac{3}{q}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}(h+f)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{f}\nabla_{x}(h+f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Proof.

For the stationary problem (
1.7
), using the Schauder estimates and Morrey’s inequality, together with (
2.49
) in Lemma
9
, we derive

(2.64)

\|\phi_{h}\|_{C^{2,1-\frac{3}{q}}_{x}}\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{C^{0,1-\frac{3}{q}}_{x}}\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{W^{1,q}_{x}}\lesssim_{\Omega}\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big\|_{L^{q}_{x}}.

Moreover, applying Hölder’s inequality with
p<3<q
, we obtain

(2.65)

\begin{split}\int_{\Omega}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}h|\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big|^{q}\mathrm{d}x&\lesssim\int_{\Omega}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}h|^{\frac{p}{q}}|\nabla_{x}h|^{\frac{q-p}{q}}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big|^{q}\mathrm{d}x\\
&\lesssim\int_{\Omega}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}h|^{p}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{q}{q}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}h|^{\frac{q-p}{q-1}}\mu^{1/2}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{\frac{(q-1)q}{q}}\mathrm{d}x\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}^{p}\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{q-p}\sup_{x}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\mu^{1/2}(v)}{\big(\alpha_{h}(x,v)\big)^{\frac{q-p}{q-1}}}\mathrm{d}v\Big)^{q-1}\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}^{p}\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{q-p}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}^{q}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{q},\end{split}

where we apply Lemma
16
with
\frac{q-p}{q-1}<1
in the last line.
Combining (
2.64
) and (
2.65
), we conclude the stationary case.
We omit the dynamical problem (
1.17
) case, as it is similar to the stationary case.
∎

We now use an interpolation argument, together with Lemmas
9
and
18
, to obtain
C^{2}
control of
\phi_{f}
.

Lemma 19
.

For
2<p<3
and
0<\beta<\frac{1}{10}
, the following
C^{2}
estimate holds:

\|\phi_{f}\|_{C_{x}^{2}}\lesssim o(1)\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}}+\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{1,1-10\beta}_{x}}.

In particular,

(1)

For the stationary problem (
1.7
),

\|\phi_{h}\|_{C_{x}^{2}}\lesssim o(1)\big(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(2)

For the dynamical problem (
1.17
),

\|\phi_{f}\|_{C_{x}^{2}}\lesssim o(1)\big(\|\nabla_{x}(h+f)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{f}\nabla_{x}(h+f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Proof.

From Lemmas
9
and
18
, it suffices to show that there exists a constant
0<\varepsilon\ll 1
such that

(2.66)

\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{1,1-10\beta}_{x}}.

Let
\tilde{\Omega}
be an open bounded subset of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
that contains the closure of
\Omega
. Suppose
\phi_{f}(x)\in C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\Omega)
. From the standard extension theorem, there exists a function
\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\in C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})
such that

\begin{split}\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)=0\ \text{ for every $x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}$}\ \text{ and }\ \tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)=\phi_{f}(x)\ \text{ for every $x\in\Omega$}.\end{split}

Moreover, it satisfies that

\begin{split}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{1,1-10\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}\leq\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{1,1-10\beta}_{x}(\Omega)}\ \text{ and }\ \|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}\leq\|\phi_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\Omega)}.\end{split}

Consider two arbitrary points
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we compute that for any
0\leq s\leq 1
,

\begin{split}&\ \ \ \ \big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\\
&=\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\big(\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\big)\\
&=\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-|(1-s)x+sy-x|^{\beta}\frac{\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\big(\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\big)}{|(1-s)x+sy-x|^{\beta}}\\
&=\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-s^{\beta}|y-x|^{\beta}\frac{\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\big(\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\big)}{|(1-s)x+sy-x|^{\beta}}.\end{split}

Taking an integration on
s\in[0,1]
on the above, we get

\begin{split}&\int^{1}_{0}\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\mathrm{d}s\\
&=\int^{1}_{0}\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)\mathrm{d}s-\int^{1}_{0}s^{\beta}|y-x|^{\beta}\frac{\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\big(\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)-\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\big)}{|(1-s)x+sy-x|^{\beta}}\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

This further implies that

(2.67)

\begin{split}&\ \ \ \ \Big|\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\Big|\\
&\leq\Big|\int^{1}_{0}\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)\mathrm{d}s\Big|+|y-x|^{\beta}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}\int^{1}_{0}s^{\beta}\mathrm{d}s\\
&\leq\Big|\frac{1}{|y-x|}\int^{1}_{0}\big((y-x)\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}((1-s)x+sy)\mathrm{d}s\Big|+\frac{1}{1+\beta}|y-x|^{\beta}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}\\
&=\Big|\frac{1}{|y-x|}\big(\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(y)-\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\big)\Big|+\frac{1}{1+\beta}|y-x|^{\beta}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{|y-x|^{10\beta}}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{1,1-10\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}+\frac{1}{1+\beta}|y-x|^{\beta}\|\tilde{\phi}_{f}\|_{C^{2,\beta}_{x}(\tilde{\Omega})}.\end{split}

Let
0<\varepsilon\ll 1
. We consider all
y
such that
|y-x|=\varepsilon^{1/\beta}
. From the directional derivative, we obtain

\Big|\big(\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla\big)\nabla\tilde{\phi}_{f}(x)\Big|\lesssim\|\nabla^{2}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ \text{ for all $x,y\in\Omega$}.

Combining this with (
2.67
), we conclude (
2.66
).
∎

3.
A priori
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate

In this section, we establish a priori
L^{2}
and
L^{\infty}
estimates for the stationary problem (
1.7
) in Proposition
5
and Proposition
6
, respectively.
These estimates will be used in the well-posedness arguments in Section
6
and Section
7
.

Throughout this section, when applying the lemmas from Section
2
, we replace
f,\phi_{f}
by the stationary solution
h,\phi_{h}
defined in (
1.7
).
We also recall from
[
17
]
that the kernel of the linearized collision operator
\mathcal{L}
is given by

\ker(\mathcal{L})=\spn\left\{\sqrt{\mu},\ v\sqrt{\mu},\ \frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}.

Denote by
\mathbf{P}
the projection onto
\ker(\mathcal{L})
, so that
\mathbf{P}\mathcal{L}=\mathcal{L}\mathbf{P}=0
. More precisely, for any
h\in L^{2}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
,

(3.1)

\mathbf{P}h=a(x)\sqrt{\mu}+(\mathbf{b}(x)\cdot v)\sqrt{\mu}+c(x)\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu},

where

a(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v,\qquad\mathbf{b}(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}vh\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v,\qquad c(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v.

Moreover,
\mathbf{P}h
satisfies that

\|\mathbf{P}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}=\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sim\|\mathbf{P}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

Accordingly, applying the mass, momentum, and energy conservation laws to an equation corresponds to taking the velocity integration against
\sqrt{\mu}
,
v\sqrt{\mu}
, and
\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}
, respectively.

We first show an energy estimate on the microscopic component
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h
and the boundary term
|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}
.

Lemma 20
.

Let
(h,\phi_{h})
be a solution to (
1.7
). Then the following
L^{2}
energy estimate holds:

|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Proof.

By multiplying the first equation in (
1.7
) by
h
, we obtain the following
L^{2}
energy estimate:

(3.2)

\displaystyle|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\phi_{E}/4}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

(3.3)

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nu^{1/2}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|.

From (
2.49
), we control the first term in (
3.3
) by

\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nu^{1/2}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

For the second term in (
3.3
), we compute

(3.4)

\begin{split}&\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(v\cdot\nabla_{x})h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v,\end{split}

where the boundary term vanishes due to the zero Dirichlet boundary condition for
\phi_{h}
. Since
h
solves (
1.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(v\cdot\nabla_{x})h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}}{2}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\sqrt{\mu}h)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.

Therefore, the right-hand side of (
3.4
) can be combined and bounded by

(3.5)

\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h}\sqrt{\mu}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}h\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Since
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
, it follows that
|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}-1|+|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
.
Combining this with (
3.2
)-(
3.5
), we conclude the lemma.
∎

Next, we control the macroscopic component
\mathbf{P}h
in the following lemma:

Lemma 21
.

Let
(h,\phi_{h})
be a solution to (
1.7
). Then the following estimate for
\|\mathbf{P}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
holds:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Proof.

Given a test function
\psi\in L^{2}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3};d\gamma)
and
v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\in L^{2}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, the weak formulation of (
1.7
) is given by

(3.6)

\begin{split}&-\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}h(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{1}}-\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{2}}+\underbrace{\int_{\gamma}\psi h\mathrm{d}\gamma}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{3}}\\
&\qquad+\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}h\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu}}\psi\big]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{4}}+\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{5}}+\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{6}}\\
&=\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\psi(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{7}}+\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation}_{8}}.\end{split}

Recall from (
3.1
) that
\mathbf{P}h=a(x)\sqrt{\mu}+(\mathbf{b}(x)\cdot v)\sqrt{\mu}+c(x)\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}
.
To estimate the macroscopic coefficients
a
,
\mathbf{b}
, and
c
, we introduce suitable test functions
\psi_{c}
,
\psi_{1}
,
\psi_{2}
,
\psi_{3}
, and
\psi_{a}
.

(1)
Estimate for
c
.

We choose the test function
\psi_{c}
as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{c}=(|v|^{2}-5)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{c})\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{c}=c(x),\quad\phi_{c}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

Using the elliptic regularity and the trace theorem, we obtain

\displaystyle|\phi_{c}|_{H^{1}(\partial\Omega)}\lesssim\|\phi_{c}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

We now compute the terms in (
3.6
):

\displaystyle\eqref{weak_formulation}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}c\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\mu(|v|^{2}-5)|v|^{2}\Delta_{x}\phi_{c}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x=-5\int_{\Omega}c\Delta_{x}\phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x=5\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\phi_{c}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{3}|

\displaystyle=\Big|\int_{\gamma_{+}}(|v|^{2}-5)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{c})(n(x)\cdot v)h\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}+\int_{\gamma_{-}}(|v|^{2}-5)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{c})(n(x)\cdot v)f_{b}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)|\nabla_{x}\phi_{c}|_{L^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{6}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{8}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Since
\psi_{c}\perp\ker\mathcal{L}
, we further derive that

\eqref{weak_formulation}_{7}=0.

Combining with
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
, we obtain the estimate for
c
as follows:

\displaystyle\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

(2)
Estimate for
\mathbf{b}=(b_{1},b_{2},b_{3})
.

To estimate
b_{1}
, we introduce the test function
\psi_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{1}=\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{1}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+v_{1}v_{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+v_{1}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{1}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}\phi_{1}=b_{1},\quad\phi_{1}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

From the elliptic regularity and the trace theorem, we obtain

\displaystyle|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}|_{L^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\lesssim\|\phi_{1}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

This, together with
\psi_{1}\perp\ker\mathcal{L}
, allows us to compute the terms in (
3.6
):

\displaystyle\eqref{weak_formulation}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Omega}b_{1}(\Delta_{x}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}\phi_{1})\mathrm{d}x=\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\phi_{1}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{3}|

\displaystyle=\Big|\int_{\gamma_{+}}\psi h(n(x)\cdot v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}+\int_{\gamma_{-}}\psi f_{b}(n(x)\cdot v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}|_{L^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{6}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{8}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

and
\eqref{weak_formulation}_{7}=0
. Therefore, we derive the estimate for
b_{1}
as follows:

\|b_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the estimates of
b_{2}
and
b_{3}
, we use analogous test functions
\psi_{2}
and
\psi_{3}
as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\psi_{2}=v_{1}v_{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{2}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}+v_{2}v_{3}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{2}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
-\Delta_{x}\phi_{2}-\partial_{x_{2}}^{2}\phi_{2}=b_{2},\quad\phi_{2}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\psi_{3}=v_{1}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{3}+v_{2}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{3}+\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{3}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{3}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
-\Delta_{x}\phi_{3}-\partial_{x_{3}}^{2}\phi_{3}=b_{3},\quad\phi_{3}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

Analogously, we obtain estimates for
b_{2}
and
b_{3}
, and hence the following bound for
\mathbf{b}
:

\|\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

(3)
Estimate for
a
.
We choose the test function
\psi_{a}
as follows:

(3.7)

\begin{cases}&\psi_{a}=-(|v|^{2}-10)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}),\\[5.0pt]
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{a}=a(x),\quad\phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

Similarly, we have
|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}|_{L^{2}_{\partial\Omega}}\lesssim\|\phi_{a}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}
. We now estimate the terms in (
3.6
):

\displaystyle\eqref{weak_formulation}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}a\mu(|v|^{2}-10)|v|^{2}\Delta_{x}\phi_{a}\mathrm{d}v=-5\int_{\Omega}a\Delta_{x}\phi_{a}\mathrm{d}x=5\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\phi_{a}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{3}|

\displaystyle=\Big|\int_{\gamma_{+}}(|v|^{2}-10)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a})h(n(x)\cdot v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}+\int_{\gamma_{-}}(|v|^{2}-10)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a})f_{b}(n(x)\cdot v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{x}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}|_{H^{1}(\partial\Omega)}\lesssim\big(|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\big)\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}+\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{6}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|e^{-\phi_{E}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

\displaystyle|\eqref{weak_formulation}_{8}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

From (
3.7
), the difference of
\phi_{h}-\phi_{a}
satisfies

-\Delta_{x}(\phi_{h}-\phi_{a})=(e^{-\phi_{E}/2}-1)a(x),\quad\phi_{h}-\phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

The elliptic regularity and
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
imply that

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}-\phi_{a}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\lesssim o(1)\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

For
\eqref{weak_formulation}_{7}
, we compute that

\displaystyle\eqref{weak_formulation}_{7}

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})(|v|^{2}-5-5)(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mu\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-5\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mu(v)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-5\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x=-5\|e^{-\phi_{E}/4}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}-5\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot(\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}-\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})\mathrm{d}x,

and the last term is bounded by

\displaystyle 5\Big|\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot(\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}-\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})\mathrm{d}x\Big|\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}-\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim o(1)\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Therefore, we derive the estimate for
a
as follows:

\displaystyle\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Collecting the estimates for
a
,
\mathbf{b}
, and
c
, we conclude the lemma.
∎

Combining Lemma
20
and Lemma
21
, we obtain the following
L^{2}
estimate.

Proposition 5
.

Let
(h,\phi_{h})
be a solution to (
1.7
) satisfying
\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}<\infty
. Then we have

\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}.

Finally, we apply the method of characteristics to derive a weighted
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate for
h
.

Proposition 6
.

Let
h
be a solution to (
1.7
). Assume that

\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1.

Then the following weighted
L^{\infty}
estimate holds:

\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

For simplicity, we write
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
throughout the proof.
We rewrite the first equation in (
1.7
) as

(3.8)

\begin{split}&v\cdot\nabla_{x}h-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h+\Big(e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}}{2}\Big)h\\
&=e^{-\phi_{E}}Kh-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h).\end{split}

Since
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
and
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
, the damping factor admits the lower bound

(3.9)

e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}}{2}\geq(1-o(1))\nu(v)-o(1)|v|\geq\frac{\nu(v)}{2}.

Moreover, since
\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
and
\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}>0
, we may choose
t>0
such that

(3.10)

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}t+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(t)^{2}e^{t}\ll 1\ \text{ and }\ e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\ll 1.

Applying the method of characteristics,
h
satisfies

\displaystyle|w(v)h(x,v)|\leq\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{2}\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w(v)}{w(V(0))}w(V(0))|h(X(0),V(0))|

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{2}\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w(v_{\mathbf{b}})|f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w(v)}{w(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}w(u)|h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w(v)}{w(V(s))}|V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))|e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}w(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w(v)}{w(V(s))}w(V(s))e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}|\Gamma(h,h)|(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s.

Using Lemma
6
and
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
, we further simplify the characteristic formula as follows:

(3.11)

\displaystyle|w(v)h(x,v)|\leq\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}w(V(0))|h(X(0),V(0))|

(3.12)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}w(v_{\mathbf{b}})|f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|

(3.13)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}w(u)|h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}s

(3.14)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}|V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))|w(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(3.15)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}w(V(s))|\Gamma(h,h)|(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s.

For (
3.11
) and (
3.12
), we use (
2.42
) and (
3.10
) to get

(3.16)

\displaystyle|\eqref{chara:0}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(3.17)

\displaystyle|\eqref{chara:bdr}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For (
3.14
) and (
3.15
), we further apply (
2.50
) and Lemma
12
to obtain

(3.18)

\displaystyle|\eqref{chara:E}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

(3.19)

\displaystyle|\eqref{chara:Gamma}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)/4}\nu^{-1}(v)\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For (
3.13
), we further apply the method of characteristics to
h(X(s),V(s))
. In particular, we denote

\displaystyle V(s^{\prime},s):=V(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u),\qquad X(s^{\prime},s):=X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u).

Thus, we obtain that

\displaystyle\eqref{chara:K}\leq\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}

(3.20)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\geq s}e^{-\int^{s}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}w(V(0,s))h(X(0,s),V(0,s))\mathrm{d}s

(3.21)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)<s}e^{-\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}w(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))h(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))

(3.22)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}w(u^{\prime})h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

(3.23)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}V(s^{\prime},s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s^{\prime},s))w(V(s^{\prime},s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s^{\prime},s))}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

(3.24)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}w(V(s^{\prime},s))\Gamma(h,h)(X(s^{\prime},s),V(s^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\Big].

Applying (
2.53
) to control the
\mathrm{d}u
integration, we bound the terms (
3.20
), (
3.21
), (
3.23
), and (
3.24
) by estimates analogous to (
3.16
), (
3.17
), (
3.18
), and (
3.19
), so that

(3.25)

|\eqref{K_0}|+|\eqref{K_bdr}|+|\eqref{K_E}|+|\eqref{K_Gamma}|\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wf_{b}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Finally, to compute (
3.22
), we pick
\delta
and
N
such that

0<\delta\ll 1\ll N,

and we consider four cases: (1)
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
, (2)
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u^{\prime}|>N
, (3)
|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u|>N
, (4)
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
, and
|u-V(s)|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u|\leq N
.

Case 1:
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
.

Using Lemma
11
, we have

\displaystyle|\eqref{K_K}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}|\lesssim\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}

\displaystyle\quad\times\int^{s}_{s-\delta}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{0}\mathrm{d}se^{-(t-s)/4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\int^{s}_{s-\delta}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-(s-s^{\prime})/4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\delta\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 2:
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u^{\prime}|>N
.

Similar to Case 1, we apply Lemma
11
to obtain

|\eqref{K_K}\mathbf{1}_{|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u^{\prime}|>N}|\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 3:
|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u|>N
.

By Lemma
11
and analogous computations, we obtain

|\eqref{K_K}\mathbf{1}_{|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u|>N}|\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 4:
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
, and
|u-V(s)|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u|\leq N
.

In this case, using (
2.55
), we obtain

\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\mathbf{k}(V(s,s^{\prime}),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}w(u^{\prime})\leq C_{N}.

We now apply the change of variables

u\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\mapsto y=X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u)\in\Omega.

By Lemma
2
and (
3.10
), the Jacobian determinant satisfies

\displaystyle\Big|\det\Big(\frac{\partial X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u)}{\partial u}\Big)\Big|\gtrsim|s-s^{\prime}|^{3}\gtrsim\delta^{3}.

Then we apply Hölder’s inequality to obtain

(3.26)

\begin{split}&|\eqref{K_K}\mathbf{1}_{|u-V(s)|>\frac{1}{N},\ |u|\leq N,s^{\prime}<s-\delta\text{ and }|u^{\prime}|<N,\ |V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|>\frac{1}{N}}|\\
&\lesssim\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}\mathrm{d}se^{-(t-s)/2}\int^{s-\delta}_{0}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\frac{1}{\delta^{3}}e^{-(s-s^{\prime})/2}\int_{\Omega}\mathrm{d}y\int_{|u^{\prime}|<N}|h(y,u^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\lesssim_{\delta,N}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Collecting the estimates from all four cases, we obtain the bound for (
3.22
) as follows:

|\eqref{K_K}|\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

This, together with (
3.25
), implies that

|\eqref{chara:K}|\lesssim o(1)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Further, combining the above estimate with (
3.16
), (
3.17
), (
3.18
), and (
3.19
), we obtain

(3.27)

\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

On the other hand, using the assumption
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
, together with Lemma
12
and Proposition
5
, we derive

\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h,h)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

This further implies that

\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Combining this with (
3.27
), we conclude the proof of the proposition.
∎

4.
A priori
W^{1,p}
estimate for
p<3

In this section, we establish a priori weighted
W^{1,p}
estimate for
p<3
in Proposition
7
. This is one of the key ingredients in the well-posedness analysis in Sections
6
and
7
.
Another key set of
C^{1}
estimates will be derived in Section
5
.

Throughout this section, when applying the lemmas from Section
2
, we replace
f,\phi_{f}
by the stationary solution
h,\phi_{h}
defined in (
1.7
), and for the kinetic weight lemmas from Section
2.3
, we replace
\alpha(t,x,v)
by the stationary weight
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
defined in (
1.12
).

Proposition 7
.

Let
h
be a solution to (
1.7
), and let
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
be the kinetic weight in (
1.12
). Assume that

(4.1)

\begin{split}&\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\infty,\\
&\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1.\end{split}

Then for
2<p<3
, the following weighted
W^{1,p}
estimate holds:

(4.2)

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

For simplicity, we write
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
throughout the proof.
Using the assumption (
4.1
) and following (
3.10
), we may choose
t>0
such that

(4.3)

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}t+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(t)^{2}e^{t}\ll 1\ \text{ and }\ e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\ll 1.

For any
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t
, Lemma
2
implies that

(4.4)

|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|+|\partial_{x,v}V(s)|\lesssim e^{o(1)(t-s)}.

Analogous to (
3.8
) and (
3.9
) in the proof of Proposition
6
, we define the damping factor
\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))
by

\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s)):=e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\nu(V(s))-\frac{V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))}{2}.

The assumption (
4.1
) and Lemma
10
imply that

\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))>\frac{\nu(V(s))}{2}>\frac{\nu_{0}}{2}\ \text{ for }\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t.

Moreover, using Lemma
10
, Lemma
19
, and (
4.4
), we bound
|\nabla_{x}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))|
by

(4.5)

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))|&\lesssim|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))||\nabla_{x}X(s)||\nu(V(s))|+|\nabla_{v}\nu(V(s))||\nabla_{x}V(s)|\\
&\qquad+|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{h}(X(s))||\nabla_{x}X(s)||V(s)|+|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))||\nabla_{x}V(s)|\\
&\lesssim e^{o(1)(t-s)}(1+|v|)\big(o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+1\big).\end{split}

Analogous to the proof of Proposition
6
, applying the method of characteristics to (
1.7
), we get

(4.6)

\begin{split}h(x,v)=&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}h(X(0),V(0))\\
&+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s\\
&+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s\\
&+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

Taking the derivative of (
4.6
) and multiplying both sides by
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)
, we further obtain

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h(x,v)

(4.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))[\partial_{x,v}X(0)\partial_{x}h(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{x,v}V(0)\cdot\nabla_{v}h(X(0),V(0))]

(4.8)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\int^{t}_{0}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))h(X(0),V(0))

(4.9)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(4.10)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}\tilde{\nu}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(4.11)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{x}_{p^{1},i}^{1}\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p^{1},i}^{1}}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})+\partial_{x,v}v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big]

(4.12)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}[h(X(s),u)]\mathrm{d}s

(4.13)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)

(4.14)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s

(4.15)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})}\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}},u)f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},u)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))]\mathrm{d}\tau

(4.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

(4.17)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})/2}\Gamma(f_{b},f_{b})(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(4.18)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))]\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

(4.19)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\partial_{x,v}[\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s

(4.20)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)}{2}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

(4.21)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\partial_{x,v}[\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))]e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(4.22)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))]\mathrm{d}\tau

(4.23)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(4.24)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(4.25)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot V(s)e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))

(4.26)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)}{2}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s.

We note that such a piecewise derivative formula gives a weak derivative of (
4.6
); see
[
26
,
5
]
.
Using (
4.3
) and Lemma
6
, we bound the weighted exponential factors appearing on the right-hand side as follows. For any
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t
,

(4.27)

\begin{split}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}&\lesssim e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}.\end{split}

We now split the terms (
4.7
)–(
4.26
) on the right-hand side into four parts, denoted by
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}
, as follows:

(4.28)

\begin{split}\mathcal{A}(x,v)&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_2}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_K}+\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_phi_E}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_K}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_gamma}+\eqref{rchara:gamma_nabla_phi_E}\\
&\qquad+\eqref{rchara:nabla_E}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_V_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_mu_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_phi_E_phi},\\
\mathcal{B}(x,v)&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma},\\
\mathcal{C}(x,v)&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb}+\eqref{rchara:bdr}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_int}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_gamma}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_phi},\\
\mathcal{D}(x,v)&=\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}.\end{split}

First, we estimate
\mathcal{A}
, which corresponds to the terms without singularity. All terms in
\mathcal{A}
contain exponential weights in
v
. Using Lemma
6
, we obtain

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))|h(X(0),V(0))|+w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})|f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big)w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v),

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}+w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\sqrt{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu^{-1/4}(v).

From Lemma
11
and Lemma
12
, we have

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)+\nabla_{v}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)]h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))w^{-1}(V(s))\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}[1+\frac{1}{|V(s)-u|}]\mathrm{d}u\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v),

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))|\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))|

\displaystyle<w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))|\nu^{-1}(V(s))w(V(s))\Gamma(h,h)(X(s),V(s))|\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Moreover, using (
4.4
) and Lemma
19
, we bound the term
\partial_{x,v}[\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))]
in (
4.21
) by

\displaystyle|\partial_{x,v}[\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(X(s))]|=|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{h}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\lesssim[o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]e^{o(1)(t-s)}.

Combining these estimates with Lemma
9
, we conclude that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)(1+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}})

(4.29)

\displaystyle\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)\big(o(1)(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big),

(4.30)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{A}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Second, we estimate
\mathcal{B}
, which corresponds to the terms involving derivatives of
h
.
For (
4.7
), we use the change of variables in Lemma
4
, (
4.3
), and (
4.27
) to compute

(4.31)

\begin{split}\|\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\|e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{X(0)\in\Omega}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{x,v}h(X(0),V(0))\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For (
4.19
), we analyze the term
\partial_{x,v}\Gamma(h,h)
. By Lemma
12
, we decompose it as

\partial_{x,v}\Gamma(h,h)=\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(h,\partial_{x,v}h)-\Gamma_{\text{loss}}(h,\partial_{x,v}h)+\Gamma(\partial_{x,v}h,h)+\Gamma_{x,v}(h,h).

Using Lemma
6
and Lemma
12
, the contribution of
\Gamma_{\text{loss}}(h,\partial_{x,v}h)
in (
4.19
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\Big\|\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\nu(V(s))w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\int^{t}_{0}e^{-(\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p})\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\nu^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p}}(V(s))w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\Big(\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}|\nu(V(s))||w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),V(s))|^{p}\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big(\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}\nu(v)|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h(x,v)|^{p}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}},

where in the third line we apply Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}s
, and in the last line we use the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
in Lemma
4
.
Using Lemma
6
and Lemma
12
, the contribution of
\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(h,\partial_{x,v}h)+\Gamma(\partial_{x,v}h,h)
in (
4.19
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\|\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u}}\mathrm{d}s\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-(\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p})\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\nu^{-1/p^{\prime}}(V(s))\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\mathrm{d}s\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

(4.32)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\nu^{-1/p^{\prime}}(v)\nu^{-1/p^{\prime}}(v)\Big(\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)\|_{L^{p}_{u}}^{p}\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}},

where in the second line we apply Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
, and in the last line we apply Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}s
.
Using Lemma
6
again, we obtain that

(
4.32
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{x,v,u}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)|^{p}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{x,v,u}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}}(v)e^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(x,u)|^{p}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big(\int_{x,v,u}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}-1}(v)|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(x,u)|^{p}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{1/p}

(4.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}},

where in the second line we use the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
in Lemma
4
, and in the last line the
\mathrm{d}v
integration is bounded since
2<p<3
, which implies
2p/p^{\prime}+1>3
.
We remark that we do not directly apply Minkowski’s inequality in
L^{p}_{x,v}
at the beginning, since sufficient decay in
\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}-1}(v)
is needed to control the
\mathrm{d}v
integral. Such decay can only be extracted by applying Hölder’s inequality successively.

From Lemma
12
, the contribution of
\Gamma_{v}(h,h)
in (
4.19
) is controlled by
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}
via the gain from the weight
w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v)
, while
\Gamma_{x}(h,h)=0
follows from the Carleman representation.
Combining all estimates for (
4.19
) with (
4.31
), we conclude that

(4.34)

\displaystyle\|\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(4.35)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{B}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Third, we estimate
\mathcal{C}
, which corresponds to the terms containing the exponential weight
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})
and the singular term
\frac{1}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|}
arising from the derivatives of the backward exit time, position, and velocity in Lemma
3
.
For (
4.11
), we control the boundary derivative terms by

\displaystyle|\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\lesssim w^{-1}(v_{\mathbf{b}})|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For (
4.15
) and (
4.17
), using Lemma
11
and Lemma
12
, we derive that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}},u)f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},u)

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}w^{-1}(v_{\mathbf{b}}),

\displaystyle\big|\Gamma(f_{b},f_{b})(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\big|
