\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}^{2}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}).

For the remaining terms in
\mathcal{C}
, applying Lemma
3
, Lemma
15
, and (
4.27
), we obtain that

(4.36)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{C}(x,v)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big]\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/4}}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{C}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}})\Big\|e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/4}\frac{1}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

(4.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Finally, we estimate
\mathcal{D}=\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}
, which contains the most challenging term
\partial_{x,v}[h(X(s),u)]
. We begin by rewriting it as

(4.38)

\begin{split}\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}&=\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\partial_{x,v}X(s)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

We expand
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h(X(s),u)
in (
4.38
) using the characteristic formula (
4.7
)-(
4.26
), with
(t,x,v)
replaced by
(s,X(s),u)
. In particular, we denote

V(s^{\prime},s):=V(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u),\qquad X(s^{\prime},s):=X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u).

Analogous to (
4.28
), the resulting expression can be decomposed into four parts. For simplicity, we denote them by
\mathcal{A}(X(s),u),\mathcal{B}(X(s),u),\mathcal{C}(X(s),u),\mathcal{D}(X(s),u)
, with a slight abuse of notation. Further, we decompose (
4.38
) as

\eqref{k_nabla_express}=\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{A}}+\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{B}}+\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{C}}+\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{D}}.

For
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{A}}
, we follow (
4.29
) to obtain

\displaystyle|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\mathcal{A}(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim[o(1)(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

(4.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim[o(1)(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(v),

where in the last line we apply Lemma
11
and Lemma
2
. This further implies that

(4.40)

\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{A}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{B}}
, recall from
\mathcal{B}
in (
4.28
) that
\mathcal{B}=\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}
.
Accordingly, we decompose
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{B}}
as

\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{B}}=\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}}+\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}}.

For the first component
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}}
, we estimate it by

\displaystyle\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}e^{-\frac{\nu(u)s}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)[|\partial_{x}h(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))|+|\nabla_{v}h(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))|]\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\Big\|\nu^{-1}(v)\|\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\Big\|\nu^{-1-1/p^{\prime}}(v)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

(4.41)

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}},

where in the last line we use the change of variables
(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))\mapsto(x,u)
in Lemma
4
, and the
\mathrm{d}v
integration is bounded since
2<p<3
, which implies
p/p^{\prime}+p>3
.
For the second component
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}}
, we apply (
4.34
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\|\int_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}(x,u)\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\|\nu^{-1}(v)\|\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u}}\|\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}(x,u)\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nu^{-1-1/p^{\prime}}(v)\|\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}(x,u)\|_{L^{p}_{x,u}}\|_{L^{p}_{v}}\lesssim\|\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}(x,u)\|_{L^{p}_{x,u}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

where we apply the same method as in the estimate for
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}}
.
This, together with (
4.41
), shows that

(4.42)

\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{B}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{C}}
, we follow (
4.27
) and (
4.36
) to obtain

\begin{split}&\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{C}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\big[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big]\Big\|\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)<s}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\partial_{x,v}X(s)\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))/4}}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]\Big\|\int^{t}_{0}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)<s}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{8}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{8}}(u)}\frac{w^{\frac{1}{8}}(u)}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}\frac{w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}{4}}}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Applying Minkowski’s inequality in
L^{p}_{x,v}
and the Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
, we further obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{C}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)}\Big\|w^{-\frac{1}{16}}(V(s))\frac{w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}{4}}}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v,u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)}\Big\|w^{-\frac{1}{16}}(v)\frac{w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}{4}}}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v,u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]\Big\|\frac{w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}{4}}}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,u}}

(4.43)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

where in the third line we use the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
in Lemma
4
, and in the last line we apply Lemma
15
with
p<3
.
We remark that, with the extra exponential weight
w^{-\frac{1}{16}}(v)
, the computation can be simplified (compared to (
4.33
)) by directly applying Minkowski’s inequality.

For
\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{D}}
, which is the most delicate part, we write out the full expression:

(4.44)

\begin{split}\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{D}}&=\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\partial_{x,v}X(s)\\
&\qquad\times\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s^{\prime},s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})}\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime}).\end{split}

To compute (
4.44
), we pick
\delta
and
N
such that

0<\delta\ll 1\ll N,

and we consider three cases: (1)
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
, (2)
|u|<N^{-1}
, (3)
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
and
|u|\geq N^{-1}
.

Case 1:
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
.

Using (
4.4
) and (
4.27
), we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\Big\|\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int^{s}_{s-\delta}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

Similar to (
4.33
), we need sufficient decay in the velocity weights to control the
\mathrm{d}v
and
\mathrm{d}u
integrations. Since
2<p<3
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\varepsilon>0
such that

\frac{2p}{p^{\prime}}+1>(2-\varepsilon)\frac{p}{p^{\prime}}+1>3.

This implies the integrability of
\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}-1}(v)
and
\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}-1}(u)
with respect to
\mathrm{d}v
and
\mathrm{d}u
, respectively.
Thus, we consecutively apply Hölder’s inequality in the order
\mathrm{d}u^{\prime},\mathrm{d}s^{\prime},\mathrm{d}u,\mathrm{d}s
, and compute

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\Big\|\int^{t}_{0}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\int^{s}_{s-\delta}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-(\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p})\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big\|\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u^{\prime}}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\|_{L^{p}_{u^{\prime}}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\delta^{\varepsilon/p^{\prime}}\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime},s))

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\int^{s}_{s-\delta}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u)\|_{L^{p}_{u^{\prime}}}^{p}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\Big\|\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\int_{u}\int^{s}_{0}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime},s))e^{-p\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\|^{p}_{L^{p}_{u^{\prime}}}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-(\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{p})\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\nu^{-1/p^{\prime}}(V(s))

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\int_{u}\int^{s}_{0}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime},s))e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\|^{p}_{L^{p}_{u^{\prime}}}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\Big\|\nu^{-2/p^{\prime}}(V(s))\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\int_{u}\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime},s))e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\|^{p}_{L^{p}_{u^{\prime}}}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\Big(\iint_{x,v,u,u^{\prime}}\int_{0}^{t}\int^{s}_{0}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}}(V(s))\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime},s))e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|^{p}\Big)^{1/p}.

We now apply the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
, so that

(X(s^{\prime},s),V(s^{\prime},s))\to(X(s^{\prime};s,x,u),V(s^{\prime};s,x,u)).

We then apply a further change of variables
(X(s^{\prime};s,x,u),V(s^{\prime};s,x,u))\mapsto(x,u)
and obtain

(4.45)

\begin{split}&\|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\Big(\iint_{x,v,u,u^{\prime}}\int_{0}^{t}\int^{s}_{0}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}}(v)\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(V(s^{\prime};s,x,u))e^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}\\
&\qquad\times e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime};s,x,u))(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime};s,x,u),u^{\prime})|^{p}\Big)^{1/p}\\
&\lesssim o(1)\Big(\iint_{x,v,u,u^{\prime}}\int_{0}^{t}\int^{s}_{0}\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}}(v)\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}}(u)e^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}e^{-\frac{\nu(u)(s-s^{\prime})}{2}}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(x,u^{\prime})|^{p}\Big)^{1/p}\\
&\lesssim o(1)\Big(\iint_{x,v,u,u^{\prime}}\nu^{-2p/p^{\prime}-1}(v)\nu^{-(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}-1}(u)|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(x,u^{\prime})|^{p}\Big)^{1/p}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

where in the last line the
\mathrm{d}v
and
\mathrm{d}u
integration are bounded due to
2p/p^{\prime}+1>(2-\varepsilon)p/p^{\prime}+1>3
.

Case 2:
|u|<N^{-1}
.

We apply the same computation as in the case
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
and derive

(4.46)

\|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{|u|<N^{-1}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

Here, the
o(1)
term arises from Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
and Lemma
11
, namely,

\Big\|\mathbf{1}_{|u|<N^{-1}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p^{\prime}}_{u}}\lesssim o(1)\nu^{-1/p^{\prime}}(V(s)).

Case 3:
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
and
|u|\geq N^{-1}
.

We consider the following change of variables:

(4.47)

\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})=\nabla_{u}[h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})](\partial_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}.

Since
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
, (
2.5
) in Lemma
2
implies that

(4.48)

\big|(\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}\big|\lesssim|s-s^{\prime}|^{-1}<\delta^{-1}.

Using the change of variables (
4.47
) and canceling all the factors involving
w_{\tilde{\theta}}
in (
4.44
), we get

\displaystyle\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}>\delta\text{ and }|u|>N^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\int_{|u|>N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\partial_{x,v}X(s)\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s^{\prime},s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\times\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)\nabla_{u}[h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})](\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}.

Applying integration by parts in
u
, we further obtain

\displaystyle|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}>\delta\text{ and }|u|>N^{-1}}|

\displaystyle\leq\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}

(4.49)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\nabla_{u}[\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})]|\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})(\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}|

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}

(4.50)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})||\nabla_{u}[\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)(\partial_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}]|

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{|u|>N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\nabla_{u}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),u^{\prime})|\partial_{x}X(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),s)|

(4.51)

\displaystyle\qquad\times|f_{b}(X(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),s),u^{\prime})||(\nabla_{u}X(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),s))^{-1}|

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s^{\prime},s))\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s)|\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})

(4.52)

\displaystyle\qquad\times|h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})||\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})(\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}|

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{|u|=N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}

(4.53)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})(\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}|

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}

(4.54)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}s|\partial_{u}[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))]|\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|\partial_{x,u}X(s^{\prime},s)h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})(\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}|.

For (
4.49
), we compute that

\displaystyle|\eqref{kk_ibp_1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim C(\delta,t)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}|\nabla_{u}[\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})]|w^{-1}(u^{\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim C(\delta,t)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(v).

Here, we use Lemma
6
and Lemma
11
to obtain the following bounds for the
\mathrm{d}u
and
\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}
integrations:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})w^{-1}(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim w^{-1}(V(s^{\prime},s))\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(u),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{u}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|w^{-1}(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim w^{-1}(V(s^{\prime},s))\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{u}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim w^{-1}(V(s^{\prime},s))(1+|V(s^{\prime},s)|^{2})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\mathbf{k}_{\tilde{\varrho}}(V(s^{\prime},s),u)}{|V(s^{\prime},s)-u|}\mathrm{d}u\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(V(s^{\prime},s))\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(u).

Therefore, we conclude

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk_ibp_1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For (
4.50
), we use (
4.48
) to control the term
|\nabla_{u}[(\partial_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}]|
in (
4.50
) as follows:

|\nabla_{u}[(\partial_{u}X(s^{\prime},s))^{-1}]|\lesssim|\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s)|^{-1}|\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s)|^{-1}|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime},s)|\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime},s)|.

Similar to (
4.43
), applying Minkowski’s inequality in
L^{p}_{x,v}
and the Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk_ibp_2}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim C(\delta,N,t)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}{w^{\frac{3}{4}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}

\displaystyle\quad\times\int^{s-\delta}_{0}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(V(s^{\prime},s))[|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime},s)|+|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s)|]\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big\|w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(V(s^{\prime},s))[|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime},s)|+|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime},s)|]\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v,u}}.

We now apply the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
, so that

(X(s^{\prime},s),V(s^{\prime},s))\to(X(s^{\prime};s,x,u),V(s^{\prime};s,x,u)).

We then apply a further change of variables
(X(s^{\prime};s,x,u),V(s^{\prime};s,x,u))\mapsto(x,u)
and obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk_ibp_2}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big\|w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(v)\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(v)}{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(V(s^{\prime};s,x,u))[|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime};s,x,u)|+|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime};s,x,u)|]\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v,u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}\int_{0}^{s}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}\big\|w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(u)[|\nabla_{u}^{2}X(s^{\prime};s,x,u)|+|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{u}X(s^{\prime};s,x,u)]|\big\|_{L^{p}_{x,u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}+\|\nabla_{x}^{3}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}\big),

where in the third line the
\mathrm{d}v
integration is bounded since
p<3
, and in the last line we apply Lemma
7
and Lemma
8
.

For
(
4.51
)
, we apply Lemma
3
and obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk_ibp_3}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim C(\delta,t,N)|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))\int_{u}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}\frac{te^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)),u^{\prime})\frac{w(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}{w(u^{\prime})}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big\|\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(V(s))\Big\|\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{u}}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big(\int_{x,v,u}\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(V(s))\frac{e^{-p\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}w^{-\frac{p}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))|^{p}}\Big)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big(\int_{x,v,u}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(v)\frac{e^{-p\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}w^{-\frac{p}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))|^{p}}\Big)^{1/p}\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

where in the third and fourth lines we apply Hölder’s inequality in
\mathrm{d}u
and
\mathrm{d}s
, respectively, and in the last line we apply the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
and Lemma
15
.

For (
4.52
), we control it in a similar way as in (
4.49
) and obtain

\|\eqref{kk_ibp_4}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For (
4.53
), we apply Minkowski’s inequality in
L^{p}_{x,v}
and obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kk_ibp_5}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim C(\delta)\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{|u|=N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(u)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\|\int^{t}_{0}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}\int_{|u|=N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(u)\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}\int_{|u|=N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u\Big\|\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(u)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{2}}\int_{|u|=N^{-1}}\mathrm{d}u\Big\|\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(v)}{w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(u)\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

where in the last line we apply the change of variables
(X(s),V(s))\mapsto(x,v)
.

For (
4.54
), using (
4.5
) we compute that

\displaystyle|\eqref{kk_ibp_6}|\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\mathrm{d}sw_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{4}}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(V(s))}{w^{\frac{3}{4}}(u)}\frac{w^{\frac{1}{2}}(u)}{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int^{s-\delta}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})}{4}}w^{\frac{1}{4}}(u)\nu(u)[o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+1]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)[o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}].

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\||\eqref{kk_ibp_6}|\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}(\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Collecting the estimates from (
4.49
)-(
4.54
), we conclude that

(4.55)

\begin{split}&\|\eqref{kk}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}>\delta\text{ and }|u|>N^{-1}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta,t,N)(\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}})[1+\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}].\end{split}

Combining (
4.45
), (
4.46
), and (
4.55
), we derive that

\|\eqref{kk}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim C(\delta,t,N)[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]+[o(1)+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}](\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

This, together with (
4.40
), (
4.42
), (
4.43
), and the fact that
\eqref{kk}=\eqref{k_nabla_express}_{\mathcal{D}}
, further implies that

(4.56)

\|\mathcal{D}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+[o(1)+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}](\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Finally, using the estimates for
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C}
in (
4.30
), (
4.35
), and (
4.37
), together with the assumption
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1
, we conclude the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.
A priori weighted
C^{1}_{x,v}
estimate and
C^{1}_{v}
estimate without weight

In this section, we establish weighted
C^{1}_{x,v}
regularity and
C^{1}_{v}
regularity without the
\alpha_{h}
-weight in Proposition
8
and Proposition
9
, respectively.

Similar to Section 4, the stationary setting
h,\phi_{h},\alpha_{h}(x,v)
defined in (
1.7
) and (
1.12
) are adopted while applying lemmas from Section
2
.
Moreover, we impose the same a priori assumption (
4.1
) as in Proposition
7
.
The smallness of
\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)
in (
4.1
) ensures the existence of
\Omega_{\delta}
such that (
2.58
) is satisfied.

We start with the weighted
C^{1}_{x,v}
estimate, using the
W^{1,p}
estimate in Proposition
7
, and bootstrap to weighted
C^{1}_{x,v}
regularity.

Proposition 8
.

Suppose the assumption in Proposition
7
holds. Then the following weighted
C^{1}_{x,v}
estimate holds:

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

Since this proposition builds on Proposition
7
, we adopt the same notation and framework as there.
Throughout the proof, we write

\begin{split}(X(s),V(s))&:=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v)),\\
(X(s^{\prime},s),V(s^{\prime},s))&:=(X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u),V(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u)).\end{split}

Following (
4.3
), we choose
t>0
such that

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}t+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(t)^{2}e^{t}\ll 1\ \text{ and }\ e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\ll 1.

Moreover, we use the characteristic formula (
4.7
)–(
4.26
) for
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x,v}h(x,v)
, and adopt the same decomposition
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}
as in (
4.28
) from the proof of Proposition
7
. Specifically, we write

\alpha_{h}(x,v)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h(x,v)=\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{A}+\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{B}+\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{C}+\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{D}.

First, we estimate
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{A}
. Since
\alpha_{h}\leq\delta^{\prime}\ll 1
, it follows from (
4.29
) that

(5.1)

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{A}(x,v)|\lesssim o(1)(\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Second, we estimate
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{B}
. Recall from (
4.28
) that
\mathcal{B}=\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}
.
Using Lemma
13
, (
4.31
), and the fact that
e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\ll 1
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:nabla_0}|

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}e^{C_{0}}\|\alpha_{h}(X(0),V(0))w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{x,v}h(X(0),V(0))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\|\alpha_{h}(x,v)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

We next use Lemma
12
to estimate
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}
by

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)|\partial_{x,v}h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\Gamma_{v}(h,h)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)}{\alpha^{2}_{h}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

where in the last line we apply Lemma
17
and use
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
.
Combining the above two estimates, we conclude that

(5.2)

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{B}|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Third, we estimate
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{C}
. It follows from (
4.36
) and Lemma
14
that

(5.3)

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{C}(x,v)|\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Finally, we estimate the most challenging term
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{D}=\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}
.
From (
4.38
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)|\partial_{x}h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

(5.4)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.

Analogous to the proof of Proposition
7
, we expand
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h(X(s),u)
in (
5.4
) using the characteristic formula (
4.7
)-(
4.26
), with
(t,x,v)
replaced by
(s,X(s),u)
, and decompose the resulting expression into four parts:
\mathcal{A}(X(s),u),\mathcal{B}(X(s),u),\mathcal{C}(X(s),u),\mathcal{D}(X(s),u)
.
We further define

\displaystyle\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{X}}:=\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}|\mathcal{X}(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\ \text{ for }\mathcal{X}\in\{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}\}.

Accordingly, we bound (
5.4
) by

\eqref{k_nabla_express2}\leq|\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{A}}|+|\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{B}}|+|\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{C}}|+|\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}|.

For
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{A}}
, we apply (
4.39
) and the fact that
\alpha_{h}^{2}<\alpha_{h}\leq\delta^{\prime}\ll 1
to obtain

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{A}}|\lesssim o(1)(\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}})+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{B}}
, we use (
5.3
) and Lemma
17
to deduce

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{B}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\alpha_{h}\mathcal{B}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{1}{\alpha_{h}(X(s),u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{1}{\alpha_{h}^{2}(X(s),u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{C}}
, we apply Lemma
13
, (
4.36
), and Lemma
17
to have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{C}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\alpha_{h}(x,v)\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}_{h}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}
, which is the most delicate part, we compute

(5.5)

\begin{split}&|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}|\\
&\lesssim\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime},s),V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\nabla_{x}h(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})|\alpha_{h}(x,v).\end{split}

To compute (
5.5
), we pick
\delta
and
N
such that

0<\delta\ll 1\ll N,

and we consider four cases: (1)
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
, (2)
|u|>N
or
|u^{\prime}|>N
, (3)
|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}
or
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}
, (4)
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
,
|u|\leq N
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
, and
|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
.

Case 1:
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
.

Applying Lemma
17
and the fact that
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
, we derive

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\alpha_{h}(x,v)\big(\frac{o(1)}{\alpha_{h}(X(s),u)}+o(1)\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}o(1)\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\frac{\alpha_{h}(x,v)}{\alpha^{2}_{h}(X(s),u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 2:
|u|>N
or
|u^{\prime}|>N
.

We first consider
|u^{\prime}|>N
.
If
|u|<\frac{N}{2}
, the assumption in Proposition
7
implies that
V(s^{\prime},s)<\frac{3N}{4}
, and thus
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|>\frac{N}{4}
. This leads to

\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})}\lesssim o(1)\frac{e^{-C|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|^{2}}}{|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|}.

Otherwise, if
|u|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, we have
t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\ll 1
. By Lemma
17
, we conclude that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbf{1}_{|u^{\prime}|>N}|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Similarly, when
|u|>N
, we obtain an analogous estimate as follows:

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbf{1}_{|u|>N}|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 3:
|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}
or
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}
.

Using Lemma
17
, we have

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbf{1}_{|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<N^{-1}\text{ or }|V(s)-u|<N^{-1}}|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Case 4:
s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta
,
|u|\leq N
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
, and
|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
.

This case satisfies the same conditions as in the estimate for (
3.26
). However, instead of applying Hölder’s inequality with
\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}=1
, we use the conjugate exponents
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}=1
to obtain

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{D}}\mathbf{1}_{s^{\prime}\leq s-\delta,|u|<N,|u^{\prime}|<N,\text{ and }|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|>N^{-1},|V(s)-u|>N^{-1}}|\lesssim\|\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

We remark that, unlike in Proposition
7
, we do not perform a change of variables to the partial derivative
\nabla_{x}
or integration by parts, but instead directly apply Hölder’s inequality with the argument to (
3.26
).

Collecting the four cases, we obtain the estimate for
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}
as follows:

|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{D}}|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

This, together with the estimates for
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{A}},\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{B}}
, and
\alpha_{h}(x,v)\eqref{k_nabla_express2}_{\mathcal{C}}
, implies that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{h}(x,v)\mathcal{D}(x,v)|\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Combining this with the estimates (
5.1
), (
5.2
), and (
5.3
), and absorbing the
o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
term into the left-hand side, we conclude the proof of the proposition.
∎

Next, we establish the
v
-derivative estimate without the
\alpha_{h}
-weight.

Proposition 9
.

Suppose the assumption in Proposition
8
holds. Then the following
C^{1}_{v}
estimate holds:

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

Since this proposition builds on Proposition
7
and Proposition
8
, we adopt the same notation and framework.
Throughout the proof, we write

\begin{split}(X(s),V(s))&:=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v)).\end{split}

Analogous to Proposition
8
, we choose
t>0
such that

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}t+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(t)^{2}e^{t}\ll 1\ \text{ and }\ e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\ll 1.

If
x\in\Omega
and
X(s)\in\Omega\setminus\Omega_{\delta}
for some
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\leq s\leq t
, then Lemma
13
implies that

\alpha_{h}(x,v)\gtrsim\alpha_{h}(X(s),V(s))=\delta^{\prime}.

Hence, we concludes the
C^{1}_{v}
estimate by

|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}h(x,v)|\leq\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Therefore, it suffices to consider the case that
X(s)\in\Omega_{\delta}
for every
s\in[t-t_{\mathbf{b}},t]
.
From (
2.35
), we have
|t_{\mathbf{b}}|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\delta}{C_{E}}}
. Hence, let
\delta>0
be sufficiently small (depending on
t
), so that
t_{\mathbf{b}}\leq t
and thus

\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}=t-t_{\mathbf{b}}.

Analogous to Proposition
7
, we use the characteristic formula (
4.7
)–(
4.26
) for
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{v}h(x,v)
.
Since
t_{\mathbf{b}}\leq t
, the terms (
4.7
) and (
4.8
) are excluded in the resulting expression. We then modify the decomposition (
4.28
) from the proof of Proposition
7
accordingly. Specifically, we write

(5.6)

\begin{split}\tilde{\mathcal{A}}&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_2}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_K}+\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_phi_E}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_K}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_gamma}+\eqref{rchara:gamma_nabla_phi_E}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_E}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_V_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_mu_phi}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_phi_E_phi},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{B}}&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}&=\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb}+\eqref{rchara:bdr}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_int}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_gamma}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_phi},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{D}}&=\eqref{rchara:K_nabla}.\end{split}

Moreover, since we consider the estimate for
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{v}h(x,v)
, we focus on the
\partial_{v}
-derivative rather than the
\partial_{x,v}
-derivative, and the lower limit of the time integrals changes from
\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}
to
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}
.

For
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}
, similarly to (
4.29
), we obtain

(5.7)

|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}|\lesssim o(1)\big(\|\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\big)+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\tilde{\mathcal{B}}
, we we apply Lemma
12
and use
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
to obtain

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{B}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)|\partial_{v}h(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\qquad+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))|\Gamma_{v}(h,h)(X(s),V(s))|\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{\nu(V(s))(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\mathbf{k}_{1}(V(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}

(5.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}
, since we focus on the
\partial_{v}
-derivative, we use Lemma
5
to deduce that
\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)}{v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot n(x_{\mathbf{b}})}\lesssim 1
.The remaining computation is similar to (
4.36
), and we obtain

(5.9)

|\tilde{\mathcal{C}}|\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\tilde{\mathcal{D}}
, we apply Lemma
17
to obtain

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{D}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{2}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\alpha_{h}(X(s),u)}|\partial_{v}X(s)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

(5.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\frac{\alpha_{h}(x,v)}{\alpha_{h}(x,v)}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{h}\nabla_{x}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

where in the second line we use
|\partial_{v}X(s)|\lesssim\alpha_{h}(x,v)
for any
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\leq s\leq t
from Corollary
4
.
Collecting the estimates (
5.7
)-(
5.10
), we conclude the proposition.
∎

6.
Uniqueness of steady solution

In this section, we establish the uniqueness of the stationary problem (
1.7
) in Proposition
10
.
The proof relies on the a priori
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate and
W^{1,p}
estimate established in Sections
3
and
4
, respectively.

More importantly, Proposition
10
requires the smallness of
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
to obtain uniqueness, which motivates the derivation of the unweighted
C^{1}_{v}
estimate in Proposition
9
.
Furthermore, the uniqueness argument developed here will be adapted in the well-posedness and existence of the stationary problem in Section
7
.

Proposition 10
.

Let
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
be two solutions to (
1.7
) such that, for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle\|wh_{i}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1,

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h_{i}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1,

then
h_{1}=h_{2}
.

Proof.

Since
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
are two solutions to (
1.7
), the difference
h_{1}-h_{2}
satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h_{1}-h_{2})-(\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}+\nabla_{x}\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h_{1}-h_{2})+\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}(h_{1}-h_{2})+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(h_{1}-h_{2})\\
&=\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{2}-\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}h_{2}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}[\Gamma(h_{1},h_{1})-\Gamma(h_{2},h_{2})]-v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu},\\
&(h_{1}-h_{2})|_{\gamma_{-}}=0,\\
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h_{1}-h_{2})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\ \phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\\
&-\partial_{n}\phi_{E}>C_{E}>0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

By multiplying the first equation by
h_{1}-h_{2}
, we obtain the following
L^{2}
energy estimate:

(6.1)

\displaystyle|(h_{1}-h_{2})|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}

(6.2)

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

(6.3)

\displaystyle\qquad+\|e^{\phi_{E}/4}\nu^{-1/2}[\Gamma(h_{1}-h_{2},h_{1})+\Gamma(h_{2},h_{1}-h_{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}

(6.4)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|.

For (
6.1
), using Lemma
9
we derive that

(6.5)

\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

For (
6.2
), we again use Lemma
9
and the elliptic estimate to obtain that

(6.6)

\eqref{f_s-h_s_micro_l2_2}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the nonlinear term in (
6.3
), we use Lemma
12
to obtain that

(6.7)

\|\nu^{-1/2}[\Gamma(h_{1}-h_{2},h_{1})+\Gamma(h_{2},h_{1}-h_{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\big(\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)^{2}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

For (
6.4
), similar to Lemma
20
, we apply integration by parts and obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{\gamma}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})(n(x)\cdot v)\mathrm{d}S_{x}\mathrm{d}v.

The first term on the right-hand side can be computed using the equation for
h_{1}-h_{2}
as

\displaystyle\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}\cdot\nabla_{v}((h_{1}-h_{2})\sqrt{\mu})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\cdot\nabla_{v}(h_{2}\sqrt{\mu})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.

Thus, we have

(6.8)

\eqref{f_s-h_s_micro_l2_4}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Collecting the estimates (
6.5
)–(
6.8
), and together with
|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}-1|\ll 1
, we conclude that

\displaystyle|(h_{1}-h_{2})|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}

(6.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

For the macroscopic estimate, let
(a_{h_{1}},\mathbf{b}_{h_{1}},c_{h_{1}})
and
(a_{h_{2}},\mathbf{b}_{h_{2}},c_{h_{2}})
denote the macroscopic components of
\mathbf{P}h_{1}
and
\mathbf{P}h_{2}
, respectively.
We use the same test functions
\psi_{a},\psi_{1},\psi_{2},\psi_{3},\psi_{c}
as in Lemma
21
, but with
\phi_{a},\phi_{1},\phi_{2},\phi_{3},\phi_{c}
solving

\displaystyle\Delta\phi_{a}

\displaystyle=a_{h_{1}}-a_{h_{2}},\qquad\phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,

\displaystyle\Delta\phi_{i}

\displaystyle=b_{h_{1},i}-b_{h_{2},i},\qquad\phi_{i}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,\quad i=1,2,3,

\displaystyle\Delta\phi_{c}

\displaystyle=c_{h_{1}}-c_{h_{2}},\qquad\phi_{c}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

The estimates on
(a_{h_{1}},\mathbf{b}_{h_{1}},c_{h_{1}})
and
(a_{h_{2}},\mathbf{b}_{h_{2}},c_{h_{2}})
are the same as those in Lemma
21
, except for the following terms: for any
\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{1},\psi_{2},\psi_{3},\psi_{c}\}
,

\displaystyle\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{\mu}})(h_{1}-h_{2})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{\mu}})h_{2}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Therefore, we obtain the following macroscopic estimate:

(6.10)

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{P}(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|(h_{1}-h_{2})|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+[\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}]\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Multiplying (
6.10
) by a sufficiently small constant and adding it to (
6.9
), we obtain the following
L^{2}_{x,v}
stability estimate:

\displaystyle\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}-h_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+|(h_{1}-h_{2})|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

Therefore, when
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
,
\|wh_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\|wh_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
, and
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
, we conclude that uniqueness holds.
∎

7.
Construction of steady solutions

In this section, we construct a solution to the steady problem (
1.7
) via the following iterative sequence: for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}h^{\ell+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}

(7.1)

\displaystyle=-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell}),

(7.2)

\displaystyle h^{\ell+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}

\displaystyle=f_{b}(x,v),

(7.3)

\displaystyle-\Delta\phi^{i}_{h}

\displaystyle=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{i}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\ i\in\{\ell,\ell+1\},

(7.4)

\displaystyle\phi^{i}_{h}

\displaystyle=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\ i\in\{\ell,\ell+1\},

where the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
.

We use the a priori estimates from Sections
3
–
5
, together with the uniqueness argument in Section
6
, to derive the regularity estimates in Section
7.1
and to establish existence in Section
7.3
, respectively.
Moreover, since the construction (
7.1
) involves
\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}
on the right-hand side, it remains to establish the well-posedness of this construction. The proof is given in Section
7.2
.

Throughout this section, when applying the lemmas from Section
2
, we replace
f,\phi_{f}
by the stationary solution
h,\phi_{h}
defined in (
1.7
), and for the kinetic weight lemmas from Section
2.3
, we replace
\alpha(t,x,v)
by the stationary weight
\alpha_{h}(x,v)
defined in (
1.12
).
Moreover, we assume the inflow condition (
1.14
) throughout all statements in this section.

7.1.
A priori
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate for the construction

In this section, we establish a priori
L^{2}
and
L^{\infty}
estimates for the iterative construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
) in Lemma
24
.
The argument follows the a priori estimates developed in Section
3
.
The main difference is that the estimates obtained here are uniform with respect to the sequence
\{h^{\ell+1}\}_{\ell=0}^{\infty}
.

Lemma 22
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds.
Under the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
),
h^{\ell+1}
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.5)

\begin{split}|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

(7.6)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\delta_{0}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Furthermore, for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.7)

\begin{split}|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\\
&\qquad+\sqrt{\delta_{0}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big(\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Proof.

First, we prove (
7.5
).
From (
7.1
) and (
7.2
), we obtain the following
L^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate:

(7.8)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\phi_{E}/4}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\underbrace{\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nu^{1/2}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}}_{\eqref{est1:Unif_h_L2_1}_{1}}+\underbrace{\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}h^{\ell+1}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|}_{\eqref{est1:Unif_h_L2_1}_{2}}.\end{split}

From (
7.3
) and (
7.4
), together with Lemma
9
, we have

(7.9)

\eqref{est1:Unif_h_L2_1}_{1}=\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nu^{1/2}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

Similarly to the proof of Lemma
20
, the Dirichlet boundary condition (
7.2
), together with the Poincaré inequality and the elliptic regularity estimate, implies that

(7.10)

\begin{split}\eqref{est1:Unif_h_L2_1}_{2}&=\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}h^{\ell+1}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining (
7.8
) with (
7.9
) and (
7.10
), we conclude (
7.5
).

Second, we prove (
7.6
).
Given a test function
\psi
, the weak formulation for (
7.1
) is

\begin{split}&-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\gamma}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma\\
&+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu}}\psi\big]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.\end{split}

Similarly to the proof of Lemma
21
, we write

\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}=\big(a(x)+\mathbf{b}(x)\cdot v+c(x)\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\big)\sqrt{\mu},

and derive estimates for
a(x),\mathbf{b}(x)
, and
c(x)
as follows.
For
c(x)
, we have

\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For
b_{i}
with
i=1,2,3
, we have

\|b_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For
a(x)
, we have

\begin{split}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\delta_{0}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining
a,b_{i},c
estimates above, we conclude (
7.6
).
Finally, we deduce (
7.7
) by computing
2\times\eqref{est:Unif_h_L2_1}+\eqref{est:Unif_h_L2_2}
.
∎

Lemma 23
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds. Under the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
),
h^{\ell+1}
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.11)

\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Proof.

From the assumption that
\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
and
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
, we have

(7.12)

e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\nu(v).

We rewrite (
7.1
) as

(7.13)

\begin{split}&v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}-\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\Big(e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}\Big)h^{\ell+1}\\
&=e^{-\phi_{E}}Kh^{\ell+1}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell}).\end{split}

Define that
w(v)=e^{\theta|v|^{2}}
with
0<\theta<\frac{1}{4}
.
Similarly to the proof of Lemma
6
, we apply the method of characteristics to (
7.13
) and use (
7.12
) to obtain that

(7.14)

\displaystyle|w(v)h^{\ell+1}(x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}\big|w(V(0))h^{\ell+1}(X(0),V(0))\big|

(7.15)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}\big|w(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\big|

(7.16)

\displaystyle+\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}w(u)h^{\ell+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|

(7.17)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\big|V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}(X(s))\big|w(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(7.18)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}w(V(s))\big|\Gamma(h^{\ell}(X(s),V(s)),h^{\ell}(X(s),V(s)))\big|\mathrm{d}s,

where
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
with
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t
.
Recall that
\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}>0
for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Now pick
t>0
such that

\nu_{0}t\geq 10\ \text{ and }\ (1+t)e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

For (
7.14
), we have

(7.19)

|\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_1}|\leq e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\big|w(V(0))h^{\ell+1}(X(0),V(0))\big|\lesssim\frac{1}{4}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For (
7.15
), we have

(7.20)

|\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_2}|\leq\big|w(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\big|\leq|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For (
7.17
), from (
2.50
) we have

(7.21)

|\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_4}|\leq\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\frac{1}{8}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

For (
7.18
), we have

(7.22)

|\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_5}|\leq\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\nu(V(s))\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Finally, for (
7.16
), we apply the method of characteristics to
w(u)h^{\ell+1}(X(s),u)
again. We denote that

V(s^{\prime},s):=V(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u)\ \text{ and }\ X(s^{\prime},s):=X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u).

Following from (
7.14
)-(
7.18
), we obtain that

\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_3}\leq\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}w(u)h^{\ell+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|,

with

\displaystyle|w(u)h^{\ell+1}(X(s),u)|

(7.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\geq s}e^{-\int^{s}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}\big|w(V(0,s))h^{\ell+1}(X(0,s),V(0,s))\big|

(7.24)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)<s}e^{-\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)}\frac{\nu(V(s^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}\big|w(v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),v_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u))\big|

(7.25)

\displaystyle+\Big|\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}w(u^{\prime})h^{\ell+1}(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\Big|

(7.26)

\displaystyle+\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}\big|V(s^{\prime},s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}(X(s^{\prime},s))\big|w(V(s^{\prime},s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s^{\prime},s))}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

(7.27)

\displaystyle+\int^{s}_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u)\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\nu(V(\tau^{\prime},s))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}w(V(s^{\prime},s))\big|\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})(X(s^{\prime},s),V(s^{\prime},s))\big|\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}.

For (
7.23
) and (
7.24
), using Lemma
11
we compute that

(7.28)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_1}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\lesssim\frac{1}{4}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\\
&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_2}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\lesssim|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

For (
7.26
) and (
7.27
), analogous to (
7.21
) and (
7.22
), Lemma
11
further implies that

(7.29)

\begin{split}\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_4}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|&\lesssim\frac{1}{8}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},\\
\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_5}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|&\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For (
7.25
), pick
0<\delta\ll 1
and
N\gg 1
and consider four cases:

(a)

s-s^{\prime}<\delta
,
(b)

|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u^{\prime}|>N
,
(c)

|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u|>N
, and
(d)

|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u|\leq N
,
s-s^{\prime}\geq\delta
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}
.

Case
(a)
:
s-s^{\prime}<\delta
.

Using Lemma
11
, we compute that

(7.30)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}|\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_3}\mathbf{1}_{s-s^{\prime}<\delta}|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\\
&\lesssim\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\mathrm{d}u\int^{s}_{s-\delta}e^{-\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})/4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\lesssim\delta\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Case
(b)
:
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}
or
|u^{\prime}|>N
.

Using Lemma
11
, we compute that

(7.31)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}|\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_3}\mathbf{1}_{|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u^{\prime}|>N}|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\int^{s}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})/4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\lesssim o(1)\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Case
(c)
:
|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}
or
|u|>N
.

Similar to case
(b)
, Lemma
11
implies that

(7.32)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}|\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_3}|\mathbf{1}_{|V(s)-u|<\frac{1}{N}\text{ or }|u|>N}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\\
&\lesssim o(1)\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{s}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})/4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\lesssim o(1)\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Case
(d)
:
|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
|u|\leq N
,
|u^{\prime}|\leq N
,
|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}
,
s-s^{\prime}\geq\delta
.

From (
2.55
) in Lemma
11
,

\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\mathbf{k}(V(s,s^{\prime}),u^{\prime})\frac{w(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w(u^{\prime})}w(u^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N},|u|\leq N,|u^{\prime}|\leq N,|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}}\leq(C_{N})^{2}e^{\theta N^{2}}.

Moreover, consider the following change of variable: for fixed
s^{\prime},s,X(s)
,

u\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\mapsto X(s^{\prime},s)=X(s^{\prime};s,X(s),u)\in\Omega.

From Lemma
2
and the assumption, we get

(7.33)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}|\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_3}\mathbf{1}_{|V(s)-u|\geq\frac{1}{N},|u|\leq N,s-s^{\prime}\geq\delta,|u^{\prime}|\leq N,|V(s^{\prime},s)-u^{\prime}|\geq\frac{1}{N}}|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\\
&\lesssim(C_{N})^{2}e^{\theta N^{2}}(N)^{4}\int^{t}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\int^{s}_{0}e^{-\nu_{0}(s-s^{\prime})/4}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\int_{\Omega}\big|h^{\ell+1}(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\big|\frac{1}{\delta^{3}}\mathrm{d}y\lesssim_{\delta,N}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Combining (
7.30
)-(
7.33
), we obtain that

\begin{split}&\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}\eqref{est4:Unif_wh_Linfty_3_3}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|\lesssim_{\delta,N}o(1)\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

This, together with (
7.28
) and (
7.29
), shows that

\eqref{est3:Unif_wh_Linfty_3}\lesssim\frac{3}{8}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+2\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Combining the above with (
7.19
)-(
7.22
), we conclude (
7.11
).
∎

Lemma 24
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds.
Under the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
),
(h^{\ell+1},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.34)

\displaystyle|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.35)

\displaystyle\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.36)
