\displaystyle\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell+1},h^{\ell+1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{\ell+1},h^{\ell+1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}^{2}.

(7.37)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

We first prove (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) by induction.
From the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
) and the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
, (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) hold for
\ell=-1
.

Suppose that (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) hold for all
-1\leq\ell\leq k-1
.
Now consider the case when
\ell=k
. From (
7.7
) in Lemma
22
and (
7.11
) in Lemma
23
, we obtain that

(7.38)

\begin{split}|h^{k+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{k},h^{k})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\\
&\qquad+\sqrt{\delta_{0}}\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},\end{split}

(7.39)

\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{k},h^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

On the other hand, Lemma
12
implies that

(7.40)

\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(h^{k},h^{k})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(h^{k},h^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Computing
2\times\eqref{est1:Unif_steady}+\eqref{est2:Unif_steady}
and using (
7.40
), we derive

\begin{split}&2|h^{k+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim 2|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+3\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}+2\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\\
&\qquad+\sqrt{\delta_{0}}\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+2\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Recall that (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) hold for
\ell=k-1
.
Since
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|^{1/2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\delta_{0}\ll 1
and
\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
, we further obtain that

2|h^{k+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\frac{1}{2}\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\frac{1}{2}\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 2|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+3\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

This implies that

\begin{split}&4|h^{k+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{k+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\end{split}

and thus (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) hold for all
\ell=k
. Therefore, we conclude (
7.34
) and (
7.35
) for all
\ell\geq-1
.

Next, using (
7.35
) and (
7.40
), we conclude (
7.36
) for all
\ell\geq-1
.
Finally, we conclude (
7.37
) for all
\ell\geq-1
from (
7.35
) and
Lemma
9
.
∎

7.2.
Well-posedness of the construction

Recall that the construction (
7.1
) has
\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}
on the right-hand side. We now establish the well-posedness of the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
) in the following theorem.

Define
E_{h}^{\ell}=-\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{\ell}+\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}
.
Similar to the definition of
\alpha_{h}
in (
1.12
), we define
\alpha^{\ell}_{h}
as follows: for every
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}\alpha^{\ell}_{h}:=\begin{cases}&\chi_{\delta^{\prime}}\Big(\big[|v\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi(x)|^{2}+\xi^{2}(x)-2(v\cdot\nabla^{2}\xi(x)\cdot v)\xi(x)-2(E_{h}^{\ell}(\tilde{x})\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi(\tilde{x}))\xi(x)\big]^{1/2}\Big),\ \text{ if }x\in\Omega_{\delta},\\[5.0pt]
&\delta^{\prime},\ \text{ if }x\in\Omega\setminus\Omega_{\delta},\end{cases}\end{split}

where
\chi_{\delta^{\prime}}
and
\xi
are defined in (
1.13
) and (
1.4
), respectively.
Moreover, we set
\alpha^{-1}_{h}:=0
for convenience.

Theorem 11
.

For any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
, assume that
(h^{\ell},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})
from the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
).
Then there exists a unique solution
h^{\ell+1}
such that

(7.41)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}&-\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}h^{\ell+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\\
&=-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell}),\\
h^{\ell+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=f_{b}(x,v),\\
-\Delta\phi_{h}^{\ell+1}&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\ \ \ \ \phi^{\ell+1}_{h}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{split}

with the solution
h^{\ell+1}
satisfying that

(7.42)

\displaystyle|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}

\displaystyle\leq|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.43)

\displaystyle\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.44)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha^{\ell}_{h}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.45)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

The proof of Theorem
11
proceeds in two steps.

•

In
Step 1
, we introduce a prescribed function
g(x,v)
. Proposition
12
shows that the steady problem (
7.49
)–(
7.52
), involving a sufficiently small constant
0<\lambda\ll 1
, admits a unique solution.
We remark that the inclusion of a small
\lambda\ll 1
is in the same spirit as in
[
12
]
, where
\lambda
was used to handle the
\ell
-iteration of
K^{\ell}
.

•

In
Step 2
, Proposition
15
applies an induction argument to extend the result from
\lambda
to
1
.
By replacing the prescribed function
g
with the sequence
\{h^{\ell}\}_{\ell=0}^{\infty}
in (
7.1
), the well-posedness of the problem is established.

Step 1.

Suppose that a given function
g(x,v)
satisfies that
g|_{\gamma_{-}}=f_{b}(x,v)
and

(7.46)

\begin{split}&|g|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|g\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\end{split}

Moreover, define
\phi_{g}
by

(7.47)

\begin{cases}&-\Delta\phi_{g}=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\[5.0pt]
&\phi_{g}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

Proposition 12
.

For any non-negative
C\geq 0
, let
S_{1}(x,v)
be a given function that satisfies

(7.48)

\begin{split}\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds. Assume that
(g,\phi_{g})
satisfy (
7.46
) and (
7.47
).
Then there exists a sufficiently small
0<\lambda=\lambda(\Omega)\ll 1
such that the following system

\displaystyle v\cdot\nabla_{x}h

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h

(7.49)

\displaystyle=-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{1},

(7.50)

\displaystyle h|_{\gamma_{-}}

\displaystyle=f_{b}(x,v),

(7.51)

\displaystyle-\Delta\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,

(7.52)

\displaystyle\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.

admits a unique solution
h(x,v)
satisfying that

(7.53)

\displaystyle|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].

Moreover, the pair
(h,\phi_{h})
satisfies

(7.54)

\begin{split}\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Remark 6
.

The system (
7.49
)-(
7.52
) is a Vlasov-Boltzmann system with a given potential field
\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})
and additional source terms. Therefore, the well-posedness of the problem does not require regularity estimates for
h
, and we only need to control the term
-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}
on the right-hand side.

Proof.

The estimates in this proposition are analogous to those obtained in Sections
3
and
6
.
Owing to the similarity of the arguments, we omit some of the details.
We remark that the main difference between this proposition and the estimates in the previous sections lies in the presence of the coefficient
\lambda
in (
7.49
), and in the fact that the pair
(g,\nabla_{x}\phi_{g})
is externally given rather than determined self-consistently.

Step 1. Construction.

Choose a constant
0<\lambda<1
, we construct solutions to the steady problem (
7.49
)-(
7.52
) via the following sequences: for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.55)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{\ell+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\\
&=-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{1},\\
h^{\ell+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=f_{b}(x,v),\\
-\Delta\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\end{split}

where the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
.
From the assumption on
(g,\phi_{g})
, the system (
7.55
) is a steady Vlasov–Boltzmann equation with given potential and sources. Its well-posedness can be justified by the same argument as in
[
15
]
, where
Kh^{\ell}
was introduced for the solution’s construction. Here we omit the details of this process and keep
\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}
on the left-hand side and directly estimate the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
control of (
7.55
).
Moreover, the characteristic associated with (
7.55
) satisfies
Lemmas
8
and
9
in Section
2.1
.

Step 2.
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate.

(a)

Analogously to Lemma
20
, it follows from (
7.55
) that

(7.56)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\phi_{E}/4}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\underbrace{\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\nu^{1/2}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}}_{\eqref{eq2:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{1}}+\lambda\underbrace{\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}h^{\ell+1}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|}_{\eqref{eq2:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{2}}.\end{split}

It can be verified that Lemma
9
applies to
(g,\nabla_{x}\phi_{g})
in (
7.47
).
Hence, we have

\eqref{eq2:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{1}\lesssim\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

Following the estimates in Lemma
20
, we have

\eqref{eq2:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{2}\lesssim\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\big).

These, together with (
7.56
), implies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.57)

\begin{split}|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\big).\end{split}

Analogously to Lemma
21
, given a test function
\psi
, the weak formulation for (
7.55
) is

(7.58)

\begin{split}&-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\gamma}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma\\
&+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu}}\psi\big]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&=\underbrace{-\lambda\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{eq4:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{1}}+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}[e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{1}]\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.\end{split}

Similarly to the proof of Lemmas
21
and
22
, we write

\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}=\big(a(x)+\mathbf{b}(x)\cdot v+c(x)\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\big)\sqrt{\mu}.

The estimate on
c(x)
follow from Lemma
21
as follows:

\begin{split}\|c\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

The estimate on
\mathbf{b}(x)
follow from Lemma
21
as follows: for
i=1,2,3
,

\begin{split}\|b_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

The estimate on
a(x)
follow from Lemma
21
except the term
\eqref{eq4:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{1}
.
We choose

\begin{cases}&\psi_{a}=-(|v|^{2}-10)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}),\\
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{a}=a(x)\text{ in }\Omega,\ \phi_{a}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

Using the fact that
|e^{-\phi_{E}}-1|\ll 1
, we get

\begin{split}|\eqref{eq4:steady_small_lambda_step1}_{1}|&=\big|-\lambda\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\big|\\
&=\big|-5\lambda\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x\big|\lesssim\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\lambda\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

This further implies that

\begin{split}\|a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\lambda^{2}\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Hence, we obtain that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.59)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\lambda^{2}\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining (
7.57
) with (
7.59
), we conclude that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.60)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\\
&\qquad+\sqrt{\lambda}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|^{1/2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+(1+\sqrt{\lambda})\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

It can be verified that Lemma
12
applies to
(g,\nabla_{x}\phi_{g})
.
Using the key estimate (
7.60
), we obtain that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|^{1/2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+\lambda\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+(1+\sqrt{\lambda})\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

From the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
and the assumption (
7.46
) on
g(x,v)
, there exists a sufficiently small
0<\lambda=\lambda(\Omega)\ll 1
such that

\begin{split}|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(\lambda+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|^{1/2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

This, together with the assumptions (
7.46
) and (
7.48
), it follows by induction that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.61)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

(b)

Analogously to Proposition
6
, we rewrite (
7.55
) as

(7.62)

\begin{split}&v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\Big(e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}\Big)h^{\ell+1}\\
&=e^{-\phi_{E}}Kh^{\ell+1}-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{1}.\end{split}

From the assumption on
(g,\phi_{g})
and
\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}>0
, we get

\frac{1}{2}\nu(v)\geq e^{-\phi_{E}}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\nu(v)>\frac{\nu_{0}}{2}.

Define that
w(v)=e^{\theta|v|^{2}}
with
0<\theta<\frac{1}{4}
. We now pick
t>0
such that

\nu_{0}t\geq 10\ \text{ and }\ (1+t)e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

Applying the method of characteristics on (
7.62
) and following Proposition
6
, we obtain that

(7.63)

\displaystyle|w(v)h^{\ell+1}(x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}\big|w(V(0))h^{\ell+1}(X(0),V(0))\big|

(7.64)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,v)<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\frac{\nu(V(s))}{4}\mathrm{d}s}\big|w(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\big|

(7.65)

\displaystyle+\Big|\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w(V(s))}{w(u)}w(u)h^{\ell+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\Big|

(7.66)

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}\big|V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(X(s))\big|w(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(7.67)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}w(V(s))\big|\Gamma(g(X(s),V(s)),g(X(s),V(s)))\big|\mathrm{d}s,

(7.68)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\nu(V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}w(V(s))|S_{1}(X(s),V(s))|\mathrm{d}s,

where
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
with
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t
.

Similarly to Proposition
6
, we compute that

\begin{split}&\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_1}+\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_2}+\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_3}+\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_5}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{4}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\frac{\lambda}{4}\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For (
7.66
), from (
2.50
) in Lemma
9
we have

\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_4}\leq\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\frac{\lambda}{4}\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

For (
7.68
), from the assumption (
7.48
) we have

\eqref{eq8:steady_small_lambda_step1_6}\leq\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int^{t}_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\nu_{0}(t-s)/4}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim C[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].

Collecting the above estimates, we conclude that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.69)

\begin{split}&\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\lambda}{4}\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}]+\|h^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Applying Lemma
12
to
(g,\nabla_{x}\phi_{g})
, and using (
7.61
), (
7.69
) and Lemma
9
, it follows by induction that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.70)

\begin{split}\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Step 3. Existence.

(a)

From (
7.55
),
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.71)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\\
&=\-\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu},\\
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=0,\\
-\Delta(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{split}

From (
7.71
), the
L^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate yields

(7.72)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\Big|\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{eq15:steady_small_lambda_step1}^{*}}\Big|.\end{split}

Similarly to Lemma
20
, from (
7.71
) we have

\begin{split}|\eqref{eq15:steady_small_lambda_step1}^{*}|&=\big|\lambda\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\big|\\
&\lesssim\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\big).\end{split}

From (
7.72
), we get

(7.73)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\big).\end{split}

On the other hand, following part
(a)
of the regularity estimates (
7.58
) and (
7.59
), we obtain that

(7.74)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\lambda^{2}\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

By taking
\eqref{eq17:steady_small_lambda_step1}\times 2+\eqref{eq18:steady_small_lambda_step1}
,
(h^{\ell+1},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\big(\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}\big)\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

This, together with (
7.61
) and the elliptic regularity estimate, implies that
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
are Cauchy sequences in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
and
L^{2}(\Omega)
, respectively.

(b)

Since
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
from the construction (
7.55
) are Cauchy sequences in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
and
L^{2}(\Omega)
, respectively, there exist

h(x,v)\in L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ and }\ \nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\in L^{2}(\Omega)\text{ with }\phi_{h}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,

such that

(7.75)

\begin{split}h^{\ell}&\to h\ \text{ in }L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$},\\
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(x)&\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\ \text{ in }L^{2}(\Omega)\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$}.\end{split}

Combining the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.61
), we conclude that

|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].

Moreover, the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.70
) implies that

(7.76)

\begin{split}\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

(c)

It remains to show that
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
obtained in (
7.75
) is a solution of (
7.49
)–(
7.52
) in the sense of Definition
1
.
From the construction (
7.55
), for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
,

(7.77)

\begin{split}&\int_{\gamma_{+}}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma-\int_{\gamma_{-}}f_{b}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\\
&+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\\
&=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}S_{1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x.\end{split}

Moreover, for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
and
\varphi\in H^{1}_{0}(\Omega)\cap C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega})
,

(7.78)

\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x.

For the first line in (
7.77
), from
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
in (
7.75
), we derive

\begin{split}\int_{\gamma_{+}}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma&\rightarrow\int_{\gamma_{+}}h\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}hv\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

For the second line in (
7.77
), using the
L^{2}
convergence
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}
in (
7.75
), we further get

\begin{split}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

Moreover, the property that
K
is bounded on
L^{2}_{v}
from Lemma
10
shows that

\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.

For the third line in (
7.77
), the
L^{\infty}
estimates on
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.70
), (
7.76
) and Lemma
12
imply that

\begin{split}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

From the convergence of every term in (
7.77
),
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
solves (
7.49
) and (
7.50
).

For (
7.78
), using the
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}
in (
7.75
), together with
\varphi\in H^{1}_{0}(\Omega)\cap C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega})
, we obtain

\begin{split}\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\end{split}

and thus
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
solves (
7.51
) and (
7.52
).
Therefore,
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
is a weak solution of (
7.49
)–(
7.52
).

Step 4. Uniqueness.

Let
(h_{1},\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{1}})
and
(h_{2},\nabla_{x}\phi_{h_{2}})
be two solutions of (
7.49
)-(
7.52
) that satisfy (
7.53
).
Therefore, the equation of
h_{1}-h_{2}
satisfies that

(7.79)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h_{1}-h_{2})&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h_{1}-h_{2})+\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}(h_{1}-h_{2})+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(h_{1}-h_{2})\\
&=\-\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h_{1}}-\phi_{h_{2}})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu},\\
h_{1}-h_{2}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=0,\\
-\Delta(\phi_{h_{1}}-\phi_{h_{2}})&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h_{1}-h_{2})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi_{h_{1}}-\phi_{h_{2}}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{split}

Analogously to (
7.72
)-(
7.73
), from (
7.79
) we have the following
L^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate:

(7.80)

\begin{split}&|h_{1}-h_{2}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h_{1}}-\phi_{h_{2}})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h_{1}-h_{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

In addition, following the macroscopic estimates in Proposition
10
and Lemma
21
, we obtain that

(7.81)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h_{1}-h_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h_{1}-h_{2}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\delta_{0}\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\big(\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

where
|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}-1|\leq\delta_{0}\ll 1
.
By taking
\eqref{eq20:steady_small_lambda_step1}\times 2+\eqref{eq21:steady_small_lambda_step1}
, we derive that

|h_{1}-h_{2}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim\big(\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\delta_{0}\big)\|h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

From the assumption (
7.46
) on
g(x,v)
and
0<\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda\ll 1
, we conclude that
h_{1}=h_{2}
.
∎

Corollary 13
.

The constant
\lambda
in Proposition
12
depends only on
\Omega
and is independent of
g
and
h
.
Moreover, under the same assumptions, the proposition remains valid for all constants
0<\lambda^{*}\leq\lambda
.

Next, we prove the regularity of
h
obtained in Proposition
12
, which will be applied to study the limit of the system (
7.1
) - (
7.4
) as
\ell\to\infty
.

Proposition 14
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds, and let
0<\lambda=\lambda(\Omega)\ll 1
be sufficiently small as in Proposition
12
.
For any non-negative
C\geq 0
, assume the same hypotheses on the functions
S_{1}(x,v)
and
(g,\phi_{g})
as in Proposition
12
.
Define the weight function
\alpha_{g}
by

(7.82)

\begin{split}\alpha_{g}:=\begin{cases}&\chi_{\delta^{\prime}}\Big(\big[|v\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi(x)|^{2}+\xi^{2}(x)-2(v\cdot\nabla^{2}\xi(x)\cdot v)\xi(x)-2(E_{g}(\tilde{x})\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi(\tilde{x}))\xi(x)\big]^{1/2}\Big),\ \text{ if }x\in\Omega_{\delta},\\[5.0pt]
&\delta^{\prime},\ \text{ if }x\in\Omega\setminus\Omega_{\delta},\end{cases}\end{split}

where
E_{g}(x):=-\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}(x)-\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(x)
.
Further, assume that
g(x,v)
and
S_{1}(x,v)
satisfy

(7.83)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.84)

\displaystyle\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].

Then the unique solution
h(x,v)
to the system (
7.49
)–(
7.52
) satisfies

(7.85)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big],

(7.86)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].

Proof.

The strategy for proving this proposition follows that of Proposition
12
, and the estimates are analogous to those obtained in Sections
4
and
5
.
Owing to the similarity of the arguments, we omit some of the details.
For the reader’s convenience, we repeat the construction from Proposition
12
.

Step 1. Construction.

For any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.87)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{\ell+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\\
&=-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{1},\\
h^{\ell+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=f_{b}(x,v),\\
-\Delta\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\end{split}

where the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
.

Again, (
7.87
) is a steady Vlasov Boltzmann equation with given potential and given sources. To rigorously justify that the regularity of the system is bounded, one can again rewrite the linear Boltzmann operator as
\nu h^{\ell+1}-Kh^{\ell}
and prove the uniform in
\ell
control of the regularity. Such a process has been done in
[
5
]
. For simplicity, we skip the proof of this procedure and directly estimate the regularity of (
7.87
) in the next step.

Step 2.
W^{1,p}
estimate and weighted
C^{1}
estimate.

(a)

Under the construction (
7.87
), we claim that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.88)

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].

We prove (
7.88
) by induction.
From the initial setting
h^{0}=0
, (
7.88
) holds for
\ell=0
.
Suppose that (
7.88
) holds for all
0\leq\ell\leq k
.
We now consider the case when
\ell=k+1
.
Applying the method of characteristics to (
7.1
) and following (
4.7
)-(
4.26
) in Proposition
7
, we obtain

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}(x,v)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}

(7.89)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\big(\partial_{x,v}X(0)\cdot w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{x}h^{k+1}(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{x,v}V(0)\cdot w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{v}h^{k+1}(X(0),V(0))\big)

(7.90)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}\geq t}e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\Big(-\int^{t}_{0}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\big]\mathrm{d}s\Big)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))h^{k+1}(X(0),V(0))

(7.91)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{2}\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{x}_{p^{1},i}^{1}\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p^{1},i}^{1}}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})+\partial_{x,v}v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big]

(7.92)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\Big(-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\big]\mathrm{d}s\Big)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(7.93)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\big(-\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}\tilde{\nu}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\big)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(7.94)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\partial_{x,v}\big[h^{k+1}(X(s),u)\big]\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(7.95)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\big(\partial_{v}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\cdot\partial_{x,v}V(s)\big)h^{k+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\big(-\partial_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\cdot\partial_{x,v}X(s)\big)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(7.96)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h^{k+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

(7.97)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}(\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}},u)f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(-\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(7.98)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)h^{k+1}(X(s),u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s

(7.99)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}\big[\Gamma(g,g)(X(s),V(s))\big]\mathrm{d}s

(7.100)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}(\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})/2}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\Gamma(f_{b},f_{b})(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(-\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}

(7.101)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\Gamma(g,g)(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\cdot\partial_{x,v}X(s)\Big)

(7.102)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\Gamma(g,g)(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(V(s)\cdot\partial_{x,v}\big[\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(X(s))\big]\Big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}

(7.103)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(X(s))\Big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}

(7.104)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(7.105)

\displaystyle+\lambda\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}(\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\big(v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(x_{\mathbf{b}})\big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}\sqrt{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(-\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)\big(V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(X(s))\big)

(7.106)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\big(V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(X(s))\big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}

(7.107)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot V(s)\Big)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\lambda\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\big(V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{k}_{h}(X(s))\big)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))/2}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\cdot\partial_{x,v}X(s)\Big)

(7.108)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(7.109)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}\big[S_{1}(X(s),V(s))\big]\mathrm{d}s

(7.110)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}(\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})S_{1}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(7.111)

\displaystyle+\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\Big(-\int_{s}^{t}\partial_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))S_{1}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s,

where
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
with
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t
.

Similar to Proposition
7
, the piece-wise formula above is a weak derivative to a mild formulation of (
7.87
).
We now denote

(7.112)

\begin{split}&\mathcal{A}(x,v)=\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_1_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_2_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_K_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_phi_E_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_K_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_gamma_ell_step1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad+\eqref{rchara:gamma_nabla_phi_E_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_E_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_V_phi_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_phi_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_mu_phi_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_phi_E_phi_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_S1_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_nu_S1_ell_step1},\\
&\mathcal{B}(x,v)=\eqref{rchara:nabla_0_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma_ell_step1},\\
&\mathcal{C}(x,v)=\eqref{rchara:bdr_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_int_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_gamma_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_phi_ell_step1}+\eqref{rchara:nabla_tb_S1_step1},\\
&\mathcal{D}(x,v)=\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}.\end{split}

We remark that, compared with the expansion (
4.7
)-(
4.26
) in Proposition
7
, three additional terms appear in (
7.109
)–(
7.111
) due to the presence of
S_{1}
.

From the assumption that
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1
, Lemma
6
implies that

\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\lesssim e^{o(1)(t-s)|v|}\ \text{ for any $\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t$}.

Recall that
\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}>0
for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Now fix a constant
t>0
such that

\nu_{0}t\geq 10\ \text{ and }\ (1+t^{2})e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}}{4}t}\leq\frac{1}{10}.

Since
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1
, we further have

\big(|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big)t^{2}\ll 1.

From Lemmas
9
and
19
, together with the assumption (
7.83
) on
(g,\phi_{g})
, we have

\big(\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)t^{2}\ll 1.

Moreover, the assumption (
7.83
) on
(g,\phi_{g})
implies

(7.113)

\frac{3}{2}\nu(V(s))>\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s)):=e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\nu(V(s))+\frac{V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}(X(s))}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\nu(V(s))>\frac{\nu_{0}}{2}.

Thus, we compute that

\begin{split}&\big|\nabla_{x,v}\big[\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\big]\big|\\
&\lesssim\big(\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+o(1)[\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)(1+|v|)e^{o(1)(t-s)}+e^{o(1)(t-s)}.\end{split}

Moreover, Lemma
2
and Lemma
19
imply that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}\big|\partial_{x,v}\big[\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(X(s))\big]\big|&\lesssim\big(o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)e^{o(1)(t-s)}.\end{split}

For
\mathcal{A}(x,v)
, similarly to the estimates (
4.29
) and (
4.30
) on
\mathcal{A}(x,v)
in Proposition
7
, together with the assumptions (
7.48
) and (
7.84
) on
S_{1}
, we obtain that

(7.114)

\begin{split}|\mathcal{A}(x,v)|&\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)\big(\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big)\\
&\qquad+w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v)\big(\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big),\\
\|\mathcal{A}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\\
&\qquad+C\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

For
\mathcal{B}(x,v)
, similar to the estimates (
4.31
) and (

) in Proposition

, from Lemma
4
, Lemma

, Lemma

\|\mathcal{B}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\frac{1}{10}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\mathcal{C}(x,v)
, similar to the estimates (
4.36
) and (
4.37
) in Proposition
7
, from Lemma
3
, Lemma
15
and the assumption (
7.48
) on

, we derive that

(7.116)

\begin{split}|\mathcal{C}(x,v)|&\lesssim\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/2}}{|v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot n(x_{\mathbf{b}})|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}),\\
\|\mathcal{C}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\Big\|e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/2}\frac{1}{|n(x_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}.\end{split}

For
\mathcal{D}(x,v)
, we rewrite (
7.94
) as follows:

(7.117)

\begin{split}\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}&=\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\underbrace{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h^{k+1}(X(s),u)}_{\eqref{k_nabla_express_ell_step1}^{*}}\partial_{x,v}X(s)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

Following the estimates on
\mathcal{D}(x,v)
in Proposition
7
, we apply the characteristic formula (
7.89
)-(

) on
\eqref{k_nabla_express_ell_step1}^{*}=w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h^{k+1}(X(s),u)
by replacing

with

.
Similar to (

), we express

and we further denote that

(7.118)

\begin{split}\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}}&:=\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\eqref{k_nabla_express_ell_step1}^{*}_{\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}}\partial_{x,v}X(s)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

For
\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{A}}
, following the estimate (

) in Proposition

, we have

\begin{split}|\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{A}}|&\lesssim\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))}{4}\mathrm{d}\tau}|\partial_{x,v}X(s)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}|\eqref{k_nabla_express_ell_step1}^{*}_{\mathcal{A}}|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s\\
&\lesssim w^{-\frac{1}{8}}(v)\big(o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big).\end{split}

This further implies that

(7.119)

\begin{split}\|\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{A}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\\
&\qquad+C\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

For
\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{B}}
, following the estimate (
4.42
) in Proposition
7
, we have

(7.120)

\begin{split}\|\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{B}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\frac{1}{10}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\big(o(1)+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{10}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

For
\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{C}}
, following the estimate (
4.43
) in Proposition
7
, we have

(7.121)

\begin{split}\|\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{C}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\Big\|\frac{w^{-\frac{1}{4}}(v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}{2}}}{n(x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u))\cdot v_{\mathbf{b}}(x,u)}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,u}}\\
&\lesssim\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}.\end{split}

For
\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{D}}
, we write it explicitly as follows for clarity:

(7.122)

\begin{split}\eqref{rchara:K_nabla_ell_step1}_{\mathcal{D}}&:=\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}\partial_{x,v}X(s)\mathrm{d}u\\
&\qquad\times\int^{s}_{\max\{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X(s),u),0\}}e^{-\int^{s}_{s^{\prime}}\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}}e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s^{\prime},s))}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\\
&\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s^{\prime},s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u^{\prime})\partial_{x}h^{k+1}(X(s^{\prime},s),u^{\prime})\partial_{x,v}X(s^{\prime},s)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

Following the estimate on (
4.55
) in Proposition
7
, we have

\begin{split}\|\eqref{kk_ell_step1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C(\delta,t,N)\big(\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big)+\frac{1}{10}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+\big(o(1)\big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big).\end{split}

This, together with (
7.119
)-(
7.121
) and the assumptions (
7.46
) and (
7.83
) on
g
, shows that

(7.123)

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{D}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\frac{1}{10}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Combining (
7.114
)-(
7.116
) with (
7.123
) and using Proposition
12
, we further derive that

(7.124)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim o(1)\big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\frac{1}{5}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}.\end{split}

(b)

Following the arguments in the proof of Proposition
8
, using the characteristic formula (
7.89
)-(
7.111
) and the notation
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}
in (
7.112
), we derive that

(7.125)

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{A}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{B}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|\|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{C}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{D}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For
\alpha_{g}\mathcal{A}(x,v)
, the fact that
\alpha_{g}\lesssim 1
and the estimate (
7.114
) imply that

(7.126)

\begin{split}|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{A}(x,v)|&\lesssim o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\\
&\qquad+\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For
\alpha_{g}\mathcal{B}(x,v)
, similar to the estimate (
4.31
) in Proposition
7
, we derive that

\begin{split}|\alpha_{g}\eqref{rchara:nabla_0_ell_step1}|&\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}e^{C_{0}}\|\alpha_{g}(X(0),V(0))w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}(X(0),V(0))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|\alpha_{g}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Following Lemma
12
, Lemma
17
, and the estimates in Proposition
7
, we derive that

|\alpha_{g}\eqref{rchara:nabla_gamma_ell_step1}|\lesssim o(1)\|\alpha_{g}w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wg\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Thus, by the assumptions (
7.46
) and (
7.83
) on
g
, we obtain that

(7.127)

|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{B}(x,v)|\lesssim o(1)\|\alpha_{g}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\alpha_{g}\mathcal{C}(x,v)
, the estimate (
7.116
) and Lemma
14
imply that

(7.128)

\begin{split}|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{C}(x,v)|&\lesssim\big(\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|wS_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\frac{e^{-\nu_{0}t_{\mathbf{b}}/2}}{|v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot n(x_{\mathbf{b}})|}w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\alpha_{g}\\
&\lesssim\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}.\end{split}

For
\alpha_{g}\mathcal{D}(x,v)
, from (
7.117
) we obtain

\begin{split}|\mathcal{D}(x,v)|\lesssim\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\frac{\tilde{\nu}(X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)\partial_{x}h^{k+1}(X(s),u)|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

Similarly to the estimates in the proof of Proposition
8
, and using the estimates (
7.118
)-(
7.128
) and the assumptions (
7.48
) and (
7.84
) on
S_{1}
, we derive

(7.129)

\begin{split}|\alpha_{g}\mathcal{D}(x,v)|&\lesssim o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|wh^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+o(1)\|\alpha_{g}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

Combing (
7.125
)-(
7.129
) with Proposition
12
, we derive that

(7.130)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim o(1)\big[\|\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{g}\nabla_{x}h^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big]+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}h^{k+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

Since (
7.88
) holds for
\ell=k
, (
7.124
) and (
7.130
) imply that (
7.88
) holds for
\ell=k+1
, and therefore we conclude the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates (
7.88
) for every
\ell\geq 0
.

Step 3. Weighted
C^{1}_{v}
estimate.

Under the construction (
7.87
), we claim that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.131)

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].

We omit the details, since the proof follows from arguments analogous to those used in the proof of Proposition
9
.
Using induction, we obtain that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\sum\limits^{\ell+1}_{m=\ell}\big(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+o(1)\|\nabla_{x}h^{m}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\big)+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Combining with the estimate (
7.88
), we conclude the uniform-in-
\ell
estimate (
7.131
).

Step 4. Regularity: Proof of (
7.85
) and (
7.86
).

From Proposition
12
, there exists a unique solution
h(x,v)\in L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
to the system (
7.49
)–(
7.52
), such that

\begin{split}h^{\ell}\to h\ \text{ in }L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$}.\end{split}

This, together with the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates (
7.88
) and (
7.131
), implies (
7.85
) and (
7.86
).
∎

Remark 7
.

Under the same assumptions, Proposition
14
remains valid for all
0\leq\lambda\leq 1
, not only for sufficiently small
\lambda
.

Step 2.

To prove Theorem
11
, in the following proposition we increase
\lambda
in Propositions
12
and
14
to
1
.

Proposition 15
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds.
Assume the function
(g,\phi_{g})
satisfies that
g|_{\gamma_{-}}=f_{b}(x,v)
and

(7.132)

\begin{split}|g|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|g\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\\
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\end{split}

with
\phi_{g}
defined by

(7.133)

\begin{cases}&-\Delta\phi_{g}=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\[5.0pt]
&\phi_{g}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

For any non-negative
C\geq 0
, let
\{S_{k}(x,v)\}^{\infty}_{k=1}
be a sequence of functions satisfying that for all
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,

(7.134)

\begin{split}\|S_{k}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|wS_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Let
0<\lambda=\lambda(\Omega)\ll 1
be sufficiently small as in Proposition
12
.
Then for all
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
with
k\lambda\leq 1
, the following system

\displaystyle v\cdot\nabla_{x}h

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h

(7.135)

\displaystyle=-k\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{k},

(7.136)

\displaystyle h|_{\gamma_{-}}

\displaystyle=f_{b}(x,v),

(7.137)

\displaystyle-\Delta\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,

(7.138)

\displaystyle\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.

admits a unique solution
h(x,v)
such that

(7.139)

\begin{split}|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}],\\
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Furthermore, assume that the sequence of functions
\{S_{k}(x,v)\}^{\infty}_{k=1}
satisfies that for all
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
,

(7.140)

\begin{split}\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim C\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

Then, for all
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}
with
k\lambda\leq 1
, the corresponding solution
h(x,v)
satisfies

(7.141)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big],\\
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big],\end{split}

where the weight function
\alpha_{g}
is defined in (
7.82
).

Proof.

The strategy for proving this proposition follows that of Propositions
12
and
14
.
Owing to the similarity of the arguments, we omit some details.

We prove this Proposition by induction.
For the base case
k=1
, the result follows from Propositions
12
and
14
.
Assume that the result holds for all
1\leq k\leq n
.
We now consider the case
k=n+1
, and assume that
(n+1)\lambda\leq 1
.

Step 1. Construction.

We construct the solution to the system (
7.135
)–(
7.138
) for
k=n+1
via the following sequences: for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.142)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\ell+1}&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{\ell+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\\
&=-n\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{\ell})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+S_{n+1},\\
h^{\ell+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=f_{b}(x,v),\\
-\Delta\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\end{split}

where the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
.

Step 2.
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate, existence, and uniqueness.

Under the construction (
7.142
), we claim that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.143)

\begin{split}|h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}],\\
\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

We prove (
7.143
) by induction.
From the initial setting, (
7.143
) holds for
\ell=0
.
Suppose that (
7.143
) holds for all
0\leq\ell\leq m
.
We now consider the case when
\ell=m+1
.

Define that
\tilde{S}_{n+1}:=-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{m})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+S_{n+1}
.
Since (
7.143
) holds for
\ell=m
, it follows from the assumption (
7.134
) on
S_{n+1}
and Lemma
9
that

\|\tilde{S}_{n+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\underbrace{\big(C+\lambda(C+1)\big)}_{:=C^{\prime}}[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}].

We rewrite the first equation in (
7.142
) as follows:

(7.144)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{m+1}&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{m+1}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h^{m+1}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{m+1}\\
&=-n\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{m+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+\tilde{S}_{n+1},\end{split}

Since this proposition is assumed to hold for
k=n
with any constant
C\geq 0
, then the construction (
7.142
) is well-posed, and (
7.139
) implies that

\begin{split}|h^{m+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim(C^{\prime}+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}],\\
\|wh^{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C^{\prime}+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

(a)

Analogously to the energy estimates (
7.56
) and (
7.57
), and the macroscopic estimates (
7.58
) and (
7.59
) in Proposition
12
together with Lemma
22
, from (
7.142
) we obtain that

\begin{split}|h^{m+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{n+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+n\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\big),\\
\|\mathbf{P}h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+n^{2}\lambda^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h^{m+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|S_{n+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\lambda^{2}\|h^{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(\delta_{0}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

where the terms
n^{2}\lambda^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}
and
\delta_{0}\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}
appear in the second equation as a consequence of the same computation as in Lemma
21
.
This, together with the fact that
(n+1)\lambda\leq 1
and the assumptions (
7.132
) and (
7.134
) on
(g,\phi_{g})
and
S_{n+1}
, further implies that

(7.145)

\begin{split}&|h^{m+1}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\\
&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|S_{n+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}+\lambda\|h^{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\qquad+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|^{1/2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|h^{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big)+\big(\delta_{0}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{1/2}\big)\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}].\end{split}

Thus, the first
L^{2}
estimate in (
7.143
) holds for all
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
.

(b)

Analogously to the estimates (
7.62
)-(
7.69
) in Proposition
12
together with Lemma
23
, from (
7.142
) we obtain that

\begin{split}\|wh^{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\frac{\lambda}{4}\|wh^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\lambda\|h^{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nu^{-1}w\Gamma(g,g)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{n+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h^{m+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

This, together with Lemma
12
and the assumptions (
7.132
) and (
7.134
), further implies that

\begin{split}\|wh^{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Therefore, the second
L^{\infty}
estimate in (
7.143
) holds for all
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
.

(c)

By direct computation, the equation of
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\\
&=-n\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}-\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}-\lambda v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu},\\
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=0,\\
-\Delta(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{split}

Following the estimates (
7.73
) and (
7.74
), together with the energy estimate (
7.145
), we obtain that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+n^{2}\lambda^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{\ell+1}-\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim\big(\varepsilon+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|wg\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+n\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big(n\lambda\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}\big)\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

This, together with (
7.145
) and the elliptic regularity estimate, implies that
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
are Cauchy sequences in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
and
L^{2}(\Omega)
, respectively. Furthermore, there exists

h(x,v)\in L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ and }\ \nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\in L^{2}(\Omega)\text{ with }\phi_{h}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,

such that

(7.146)

\begin{split}h^{\ell}&\to h\ \text{ in }L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$},\\
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(x)&\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\ \text{ in }L^{2}(\Omega)\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$}.\end{split}

Following the proof of Proposition
12
, the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
implies that
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
is the unique weak solution of (
7.135
)-(
7.138
).
Finally, combining the
L^{2}
convergence in (
7.146
) with the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.143
), we conclude (
7.139
).

Step 3.
W^{1,p}
estimate and weighted
C^{1}
estimate.

(a)

Under the construction (
7.142
), we claim that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.147)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big],\\
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C+1)\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big].\end{split}

Analogously to Step 2, we prove (
7.147
) by induction.
From the initial setting, (
7.147
) holds for
\ell=0
. Suppose that (
7.147
) holds for all
0\leq\ell\leq m
. We now consider the case when
\ell=m+1
.

Recall that
\tilde{S}_{n+1}=-\lambda(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}^{m})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+S_{n+1}
.
Since (
7.147
) holds for
\ell=m
, it follows from the assumption (
7.140
) on
S_{n+1}
, (
7.139
) and Lemma
19
that

\|w\partial_{x,v}\tilde{S}_{n+1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\underbrace{\big(C+\lambda(C+1)\big)}_{:=C^{\prime\prime}}\big[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\big]..

Since this proposition is assumed to hold for
k=n
with any constant
C\geq 0
, then (
7.141
) implies that

(7.148)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h^{m+1}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h^{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C^{\prime\prime}+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}],\\
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim(C^{\prime\prime}+1)[|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}].\end{split}

Similarly to Step 2, the remaining proof follows analogously from the estimates in Proposition
14
, together with the assumption (
7.140
) on
S_{n+1}
, Remark
7
, and the fact that
(n+1)\lambda\leq 1
. Therefore, we conclude (
7.147
) for all
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
.
Finally, combining the
L^{2}
convergence in (
7.146
) with the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.147
), we conclude (
7.141
).
∎

Proof of Theorem
11
.

First, choose a sufficiently large
N\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
such that
\lambda=\frac{1}{N}
is small enough to satisfy the smallness assumption in Proposition
12
.
Assume that the function
(g,\phi_{g})
satisfies the hypotheses of Proposition
15
. Let
S_{N}=0
and it satisfies

\begin{split}\|S_{N}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wS_{N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w\partial_{x,v}S_{N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}=0.\end{split}

Using Proposition
15
and the fact that
N\lambda=1
, there exists a unique solution to

(7.149)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h&-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{g}}{2}h+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h\\
&=-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(g,g)+S_{N},\\
h|_{\gamma_{-}}&=f_{b}(x,v),\\
-\Delta\phi_{h}&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{split}

Moreover, the solution
h(x,v)
satisfies

(7.150)

\begin{split}|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\\
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\\
