\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},\\
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

Second, we replace
(g,\phi_{g})
by the sequence
\{(h^{\ell},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})\}^{\infty}_{\ell=1}
constructed in
(
7.1
)–(
7.4
), and apply an iteration argument.
From the initial setting
h^{0}=0
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h}=\mathbf{0}
, it is straightforward to verify that
(h^{0},\nabla_{x}\phi^{0}_{h})
satisfies the hypotheses of Proposition
15
.
Then, by (
7.149
) and (
7.150
), the construction
(
7.1
)–(
7.4
) yields the regularity estimates (
7.42
)–(
7.45
) for
h^{1}
.
This further implies that
(h^{1},\nabla_{x}\phi^{1}_{h})
also satisfies the hypotheses of Proposition
15
.
Repeating this argument inductively, we obtain the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in (
7.42
)-(
7.45
).
∎

7.3.
Existence of steady solutions and proof of Theorem
1

We now combine the a priori estimates and well-posedness in Sections
7.1
and
7.2
to establish the existence of steady solutions and complete the proof of Theorem
1
.

We begin by applying the uniqueness argument developed in Section
6
to show that the sequence
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
is Cauchy in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
.

Proposition 16
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
).
Under the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
),
(h^{\ell+1},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h})
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.151)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\big(\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)^{2}+\delta_{0}\Big]\\
&\qquad\times\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big]\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

In addition,
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
are Cauchy sequences in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
and
L^{2}(\Omega)
, respectively.

Proof.

From (
7.1
)-(
7.4
), the equation of
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}
satisfies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

(7.152)

\begin{split}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})&-\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\\
&=\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}-\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})h^{\ell}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}-\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\\
&\qquad+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big[\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})-\Gamma(h^{\ell-1},h^{\ell-1})\big],\\
h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{\gamma_{-}}&=0,\\
-\Delta(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})&=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1})\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\\
\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h}&=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{split}

From (
7.152
), the
L^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate yields

(7.153)

\displaystyle|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}

(7.154)

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

(7.155)

\displaystyle+\Big|\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}-\phi^{\ell}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\Big|

(7.156)

\displaystyle+\|e^{\phi_{E}/4}\nu^{-1/2}[\Gamma(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell},h^{\ell+1})+\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})]\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

First, we estimate (
7.153
).
From Lemma
9
, we have

(7.157)

\eqref{est1:h^ell_1}=\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}.

Second, we estimate (
7.154
).
By the Calderon-Zygmund inequality,

(7.158)

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}.

This implies that

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big(\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\big).

and

\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\phi^{\ell-1}_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big(\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\big).

Thus, we get

(7.159)

\eqref{est1:h^ell_2}\lesssim\big(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)\big(\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}\big).

Third, we estimate (
7.155
).
Following Lemma
20
and using (
7.152
), (
7.158
) and the Dirichlet boundary conditions of
\phi^{\ell+1}_{h}
and
\phi^{\ell}_{h}
, we have

(7.160)

\eqref{est1:h^ell_3}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Last, we estimate (
7.156
).
Using Lemma
12
, we have

(7.161)

\begin{split}&\|e^{\phi_{E}/4}\nu^{-1/2}[\Gamma(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell},h^{\ell+1})+\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})]\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|e^{\phi_{E}/4}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big(\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)^{2}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining (
7.157
), (
7.159
), (
7.160
), (
7.161
) with the assumption that
|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}-1|\ll 1
, we get

(7.162)

\begin{split}&|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\big(\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\big)^{2}\Big]\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big]\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

On the other hand, following the macroscopic estimates in Proposition
10
and Lemma
22
, we obtain that

(7.163)

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell})\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}+|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\delta_{0}\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\big(\|wh^{\ell+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|wh^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

By taking
\eqref{est1:h^ell_diff}\times 2+\eqref{est2:h^ell_diff}
, we conclude (
7.151
) from Lemma
24
.
From the inflow condition (
1.14
), Theorem
11
shows that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\ll 1.

This, together with (
7.151
) and Lemma
24
, implies that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|h^{\ell+1}-h^{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

and thus
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
is a Cauchy sequence in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
.
Using the elliptic regularity estimate, we derive that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell-1}_{h}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|h^{\ell}-h^{\ell-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Therefore,
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
forms a Cauchy sequence in
L^{2}(\Omega)
.
∎

We are now ready to prove Theorem
1
. For the reader’s convenience, we restate it below as Theorem
17
.
The positivity of the solution, however, will be proved later in the proof of Theorem
2
in Section
8.4
.

Theorem 17
.

Suppose the inflow condition (
1.14
) holds.
There exists a unique solution
h(x,v)
to the steady problem (
1.7
) such that

\displaystyle v\cdot\nabla_{x}h-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}h+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}}{2}h+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h

\displaystyle=-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h),

\displaystyle h|_{\gamma_{-}}

\displaystyle=f_{b}(x,v),

\displaystyle-\Delta\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,

\displaystyle\phi_{h}

\displaystyle=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.

Moreover,
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
satisfies that

(7.164)

\displaystyle|h|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x,\nu}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.165)

\displaystyle\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.166)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

(7.167)

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Proof.

Step 1. Regularity: Proof of (
7.164
)-(
7.166
).

Using Lemma
24
and Proposition
16
,
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
from the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
) are Cauchy sequences in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
and
L^{2}(\Omega)
, respectively.
Thus, there exists

h(x,v)\in L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ and }\ \nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\in L^{2}(\Omega)\text{ with }\phi_{h}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,

such that

(7.168)

\begin{split}h^{\ell}&\to h\ \text{ in }L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$},\\
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}(x)&\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x)\ \text{ in }L^{2}(\Omega)\ \text{ as $\ell\to\infty$}.\end{split}

Combining the uniform-in-
\ell
estimates for
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
and
\{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in Lemma
24
with (
7.168
), we conclude (
7.164
), (
7.165
), and (
7.166
).

Step 2. Existence.

We now prove that
(h(x,v),\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}(x))
obtained in (
7.168
) is the solution to (
1.7
)
in the sense of Definition
1
.
From the construction (
7.1
)-(
7.4
) and Lemma
24
, for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,
h^{\ell+1}(x,v)
is the weak solution to (
7.1
) and (
7.2
) with the field containing
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}
.
Therefore, for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
,

(7.169)

\begin{split}&\int_{\gamma_{+}}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma-\int_{\gamma_{-}}f_{b}\psi\mathrm{d}\gamma-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\\
&+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\\
&=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x.\end{split}

Moreover, from (
7.3
) and (
7.4
), then for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
and
\varphi\in H^{1}_{0}(\Omega)\cap C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega})
,

(7.170)

\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x.

For the first line in (
7.169
), from
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
in (
7.168
), we derive

\begin{split}\int_{\gamma_{+}}h^{\ell+1}(x,v)\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}\gamma&\rightarrow\int_{\gamma_{+}}h(x,v)\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}\gamma\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}(x,v)v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h(x,v)v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

For the second line in (
7.169
), using the
L^{2}
convergence
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}
in (
7.168
), together with
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we get

\begin{split}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell+1}(x,v)\nabla_{x}(\phi^{\ell}_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}h(x,v)\nabla_{x}(\phi_{h}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}}{2}h^{\ell+1}\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}}{2}h\psi(x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

Moreover,
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
in (
7.168
) and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, together with the property that
K
is bounded on
L^{2}_{v}
from Lemma
10
, implies that

\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h^{\ell+1}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}h\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.

For the third line in (
7.169
), using the
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}
in (
7.168
), together with the
L^{\infty}
estimates on
\{h^{\ell}\}^{\infty}_{\ell=0}
in Lemma
24
and (
7.165
), Lemma
12
and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we have

\begin{split}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell+1}_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h^{\ell},h^{\ell})\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(h,h)\psi\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty.\end{split}

From the convergence of every term in (
7.169
), we conclude that
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
solves (
1.10
).

For (
7.170
), using the
L^{2}
convergence
h^{\ell}\to h
and
\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\to\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}
in (
7.168
), together with
\varphi\in H^{1}_{0}(\Omega)\cap C^{\infty}_{c}(\bar{\Omega})
, we obtain

\begin{split}\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\ell}_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}\phi_{h}\cdot\nabla_{x}\varphi\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\\
\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h^{\ell}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x&\rightarrow\int_{\Omega}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\big)\varphi\mathrm{d}x\ \text{ as }\ \ell\to\infty,\end{split}

and thus
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
solves (
1.11
).
Therefore, we conclude that
(h,\nabla_{x}\phi_{h})
is a weak solution of (
1.7
).

Step 3. Regularity: Proof of (
7.167
).

Theorem
11
shows that for any
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}&\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

Thus, there exists a subsequence
\{\ell^{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
such that

w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h^{\ell^{k}}\xrightarrow{*}g\ \text{ in }L^{\infty}_{x,v}\ \text{ as }\ k\to\infty.

Recall from (
7.168
) that
h^{\ell^{k}}\to h
in
L^{2}_{x,\nu}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\cap L^{2}(\gamma_{+})
.
A standard result of integration by parts then implies that
g=w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h
. Consequently,

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim|f_{b}|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

which yields (
7.167
).

Step 4. Uniqueness.

Since the solution
h(x,v)
satisfies (
7.164
)-(
7.167
), the uniqueness follows from the proof of Proposition
10
.
∎

8.
Dynamical problem

In this section, we prove Theorem
2
, which establishes the existence, uniqueness, dynamical stability, and positivity of solutions to the dynamical problem (
1.16
).
Theorem
2
also implies the positivity of the stationary solution
F_{s}=e^{-\phi_{E}}\mu+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}h
in Theorem
1
. We refer to Remark
4
and the proof of Theorem
2
for further discussion.

Throughout this section, when applying the lemmas from Section
2
, we treat
f
and
\phi_{f}
as the dynamical solution defined in (
1.17
), and for the kinetic weight lemmas from Section
2.3
, we replace
\alpha(t,x,v)
by the dynamical weight
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)
defined in (
1.18
).
Moreover, we let
(h,\phi_{h})
be a solution to the stationary problem (
1.7
), satisfying the conclusions of Theorem
1
.
In addition, we assume the inflow condition (
1.14
), the compatibility condition
f_{0}|_{\gamma_{-}}=0
, and the smallness condition in (
1.19
) from Theorem
2
.

This section is organized as follows. Similar to the stationary problem, Sections
8.1
and
8.2
establish the a priori
L^{2}
–
L^{\infty}
estimate and the a priori regularity estimate, respectively. To conclude the well-posedness, we apply the local argument in
[
2
]
and establish local well-posedness in Section
8.3
. Finally, in Section
8.4
, we combine the a priori estimates, the local well-posedness, and a continuity argument to conclude the global well-posedness and complete the proof of Theorem
2
.

8.1.
A priori
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
estimate

In this section, we establish the a priori
L^{2}
–
L^{\infty}
estimate for solutions to the dynamical problem (
1.17
). The argument is analogous to the
L^{2}
–
L^{\infty}
estimate for the stationary problem developed in Section
3
.

To derive the estimate, we introduce the exponential time weight
e^{\lambda t}
into (
1.17
). More precisely, we consider the equation for
e^{\lambda t}f
obtained from (
1.17
):

(8.1)

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}f)-\lambda e^{\lambda t}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(e^{\lambda t}f)-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}(e^{\lambda t}f)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}}{2}e^{\lambda t}f+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}(e^{\lambda t}f)\\
&=-e^{\lambda t}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}-e^{\lambda t}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})}{2}h+e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot\nabla_{v}h\\
&\qquad+e^{\lambda t}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}[\Gamma(f,f)+\Gamma(f,h)+\Gamma(h,f)].\end{split}

We first show an energy estimate on the microscopic component
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f
.

Lemma 25
.

Let
(f,\phi_{f})
be a solution to (
1.17
) on
[0,T]
.
For
\lambda>0
, the following
L^{2}
energy estimate holds:

(8.2)

\begin{split}&\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda T}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\lambda\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+[\lambda+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Proof.

We multiply (
8.1
) by
e^{\lambda t}f
and integrate over
t,x
, and
v
to obtain the following energy estimate:

(8.3)

\begin{split}&\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|f(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\lambda\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}\\
&\quad+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\phi_{E}/4}e^{\lambda t}[\Gamma(f,f)+\Gamma(f,h)+\Gamma(h,f)]\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\\
&\quad+\big\|\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\nu^{1/2}e^{\lambda t}f\big\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}e^{2\lambda t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t\\
&\quad+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|vh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.\end{split}

Using
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
from Theorem
1
, we control the contribution of
\Gamma(f,h)+\Gamma(h,f)
in (
8.3
) by

\displaystyle\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\phi_{E}/4}e^{\lambda t}[\Gamma(f,h)+\Gamma(h,f)]\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}.

From (
2.51
), we control the contribution of
\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}
in (
8.3
) by

\displaystyle\big\|\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\nu^{1/2}e^{\lambda t}f\big\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}.

Using (
2.52
), we control the last two terms in (
8.3
) by

\displaystyle\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|vh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Finally, we compute the second term in the fourth line of (
8.3
):

(8.4)

\int_{0}^{T}e^{2\lambda t}\underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{eq1:l2_energy_dynamical}^{*}}\mathrm{d}t.

From the zero Dirichlet boundary conditions of
\phi_{f}
and
\phi_{h}
, integration by parts in
x
yields

\displaystyle\eqref{eq1:l2_energy_dynamical}^{*}

\displaystyle=\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(v\cdot\nabla_{x})f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.

Applying the equation (
1.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(v\cdot\nabla_{x})f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\partial_{t}f\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}(\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}f)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}}{2}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Omega}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\partial_{t}\Big(e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(h+f-h)\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\Big)\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\sqrt{\mu}f)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Omega}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\partial_{t}(-\Delta_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))\mathrm{d}x-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|^{2})\mathrm{d}x-\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}}{2}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v.

Combining the above equality with (
8.4
), we derive

\begin{split}&\eqref{eq1:l2_energy_dynamical}=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{2\lambda t}\partial_{t}(|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|^{2})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t-\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{2\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\sqrt{\mu}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}f\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t\\
&\leq\lambda\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\lambda T}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&\leq\lambda\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\lambda T}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},\end{split}

where in the second line we apply integration by parts in
t
, and in the last line we use the elliptic regularity estimate.
Using the inflow condition (
1.14
), we conclude the lemma.
∎

Next, we control the macroscopic component
\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f
in the following lemma:

Lemma 26
.

Let
(f,\phi_{f})
be a solution to (
1.17
) on
[0,T]
.
There exists
0<\lambda\ll 1
such that the following estimate for
\|\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}
holds:

(8.5)

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Proof.

Given a test function
\psi\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}))\cap L^{2}([0,T]\times\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3};d\gamma)
and
\partial_{t}\psi+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\in L^{2}([0,T]\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, the weak formulation of (
8.1
) is given by

(8.6)

\begin{split}&\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}f\partial_{t}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}}\ -\ \underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{2}}\ -\ \underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{3}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\gamma}\psi e^{\lambda t}f\mathrm{d}\gamma\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{4}}\ +\ \underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}e^{\lambda t}f\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu}}\psi\big]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{5}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}f\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{6}}\ +\ \underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}e^{\lambda t}f\psi\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{7}}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\psi e^{\lambda t}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{8}}\ -\ \underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}\sqrt{\mu}h\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot\nabla_{v}(\frac{\psi}{\sqrt{\mu}})\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{9}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi e^{\lambda t}(\lambda f+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}[\Gamma(f,f)+\Gamma(f,h)+\Gamma(h,f)])\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{10}}\ +\ \underbrace{\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}[f(0)\psi(0)-e^{\lambda T}f(T)\psi(T)]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v}_{\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{11}}.\end{split}

We recall (
3.1
) and apply
\mathbf{P}
to the time-dependent function
e^{\lambda t}f
to obtain

\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f=a(t,x)\sqrt{\mu}+(\mathbf{b}(t,x)\cdot v)\sqrt{\mu}+c(t,x)\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}.

Similar to the proof of Lemma
21
, we introduce the test functions
\psi_{c}
,
\psi_{b_{1}}
,
\psi_{b_{2}}
,
\psi_{b_{3}}
, and
\psi_{a}
with the additional time weight
e^{\lambda t}
to estimate the macroscopic coefficients
a
,
\mathbf{b}=(b_{1},b_{2},b_{3})
, and
c
as follows.

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{c}=(|v|^{2}-5)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{c})\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{c}=e^{\lambda t}c,\quad\phi_{c}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{b_{1}}=\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{1}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{b_{1}}+v_{1}v_{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{b_{1}}+v_{1}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{b_{1}}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-(\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}\phi_{b_{1}}+\Delta_{x}\phi_{b_{1}})=e^{\lambda t}b_{1},\quad\phi_{b_{1}}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{b_{2}}=v_{1}v_{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{b_{2}}+\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{2}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{b_{2}}+v_{2}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{b_{2}}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-(\partial_{x_{2}}^{2}\phi_{b_{2}}+\Delta_{x}\phi_{b_{2}})=e^{\lambda t}b_{2},\ \phi_{b_{2}}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{b_{3}}=v_{1}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{b_{3}}+v_{2}v_{3}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{b_{3}}+\frac{3}{2}\Big(|v_{3}|^{2}-\frac{|v|^{2}}{3}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{b_{3}}\perp\ker\mathcal{L},\\[5.0pt]
&-(\partial_{x_{3}}^{2}\phi_{b_{3}}+\Delta_{x}\phi_{b_{3}})=e^{\lambda t}b_{3},\quad\phi_{b_{3}}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}&\psi_{a}=-(|v|^{2}-10)\sqrt{\mu}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}),\\[5.0pt]
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{a}=e^{\lambda t}a,\quad\phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{cases}

From the elliptic regularity and the trace theorem, we obtain

\|\phi_{c}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+|\phi_{c}|_{H^{1}_{\partial\Omega}}\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{x}},\quad\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\phi_{b_{i}}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+|\phi_{b_{i}}|_{H^{1}_{\partial\Omega}}\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},\quad\|\phi_{a}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+|\phi_{a}|_{H^{1}_{\partial\Omega}}\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Now we choose the test function

(8.7)

\psi=\delta\psi_{a}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\psi_{b_{i}}+\psi_{c},

where
\delta>0
will be specified at the end of the proof. Similar to the proof of Lemma
21
, all terms in (
8.6
) can be controlled except for
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
,
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{8}
, and
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{11}
:

\begin{split}\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{2}&=\delta\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2},\\
|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{3}|+|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{7}|&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+o(1)[\delta\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}],\\
|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{4}|&\lesssim|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+o(1)[\delta\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}],\\
|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{5}|+|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{6}|&\lesssim[\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2},\\
|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{9}|&\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\lesssim\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2},\\
|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{10}|&\lesssim[\lambda+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|\mu^{1/4}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+o(1)[\delta\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}].\end{split}

For
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{11}
, we apply Hölder’s inequality to obtain

|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{11}|\lesssim\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|f(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

For
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{8}
, we compute that

\displaystyle\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{8}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\delta\psi_{a}e^{\lambda t}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-5\delta\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{x}e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(8.8)

\displaystyle=-5\delta\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{2\lambda t}|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|^{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(8.9)

\displaystyle\qquad-5\delta\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot(\nabla_{x}\phi_{a}-e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h}))e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

From the inflow condition (
1.14
), (
8.8
) satisfies that

\displaystyle 5\delta\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{2\lambda t}|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|^{2}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\geq\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}.

On the other hand,
\phi_{a}-e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})
in (
8.9
) satisfies

-\Delta_{x}[\phi_{a}-e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})]=e^{\lambda t}a(1-e^{-\phi_{E}/2}),\qquad\phi_{a}-e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

The elliptic regularity estimate further implies

\displaystyle\|\phi_{a}-e^{\lambda t}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Thus, we derive that

|\eqref{eq2:macro_l2_dynamical}|\lesssim\delta\|\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\delta\|\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.

Finally, we estimate
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
, which involves the time derivative term

\partial_{t}\psi=\delta\partial_{t}\psi_{a}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{t}\psi_{b_{i}}+\partial_{t}\psi_{c}.

For the contribution of
\psi_{a}
in
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
, we set
\Phi_{a}=\partial_{t}\phi_{a}
. Then
\Phi_{a}
satisfies

-\Delta_{x}\Phi_{a}=\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}a),\qquad\Phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

Multiplying the above equation by
\Phi_{a}
and integrating by parts, we have

(8.10)

\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}=\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}a)\Phi_{a}\mathrm{d}x.

For
\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}a)
, we apply the mass conservation law to (
8.1
) and obtain

\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}a)+\nabla_{x}\cdot(e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b})-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}(e^{\lambda t}f)\mathrm{d}v=\lambda e^{\lambda t}a.

By the zero Dirichlet boundary condition
\Phi_{a}|_{\partial\Omega}=0
, the Poincaré inequality shows

\|\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

This implies that

\begin{split}\Big|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}a)\Phi_{a}\mathrm{d}x\Big|&\lesssim\int_{\Omega}|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}|\mathrm{d}x+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\int_{\Omega}|\Phi_{a}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu^{1/4}e^{\lambda t}f\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}x+\lambda\int_{\Omega}|e^{\lambda t}a\Phi_{a}|\mathrm{d}x\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+[\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining with (
8.10
), we derive that

\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+[\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Therefore, we control the contribution of
\psi_{a}
in
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
by

(8.11)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}f\delta\partial_{t}\psi_{a}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t\Big|\\
&\lesssim\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}+\delta\|e^{\lambda t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\\
&\lesssim\delta[\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\delta\|e^{\lambda t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

For the contribution of
\psi_{b_{i}},\psi_{c}
in
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
, we set

\Phi_{b_{i}}=\partial_{t}\phi_{b_{i}}
,
\Phi_{c}=\partial_{t}\phi_{c}
, and they satisfy

\begin{split}-\Delta_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}-\partial_{x_{i}}^{2}\Phi_{b_{i}}=\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}b_{i}),\qquad\Phi_{b_{i}}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,&\\
-\Delta_{x}\Phi_{c}=\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}c),\qquad\Phi_{c}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.&\end{split}

Using integration by parts, we have

(8.12)

\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x_{i}}\Phi_{b_{i}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}=\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}b_{i})\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x,\qquad\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{c}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}=\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}c)\Phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x.

For
\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}b_{i})
and
\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}c)
, we apply the momentum and energy conservation laws to (
8.1
). Define

\Theta_{ij}(f):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v_{i}v_{j}-1)f\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v,\qquad\Lambda_{j}(f):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{10}(|v|^{2}-5)v_{j}f\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v.

Then we obtain

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}b_{i})+e^{\lambda t}\partial_{x_{i}}(a+2c)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\Theta_{i}((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f)\\
&\qquad-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{i}\sqrt{\mu}e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}f\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{i}\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\cdot\nabla_{v}(e^{\lambda t}f\sqrt{\mu})\mathrm{d}v\\
&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu v_{i}e^{-\phi_{E}/2}e^{\lambda t}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\mu\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{i}e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h\sqrt{\mu})+\lambda e^{\lambda t}b_{i},\\
&\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}c)+\frac{e^{\lambda t}}{3}\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbf{b}+\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{x}\cdot\Lambda((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f)-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\cdot\nabla_{v}e^{\lambda t}f\mathrm{d}v\\
&\qquad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\cdot\nabla_{v}(e^{\lambda t}f\sqrt{\mu})\mathrm{d}v=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\cdot\nabla_{v}(h\sqrt{\mu})\mathrm{d}v+\lambda e^{\lambda t}c.\end{split}

By the zero Dirichlet boundary conditions
\Phi_{b_{i}}|_{\partial\Omega}=\Phi_{c}|_{\partial\Omega}=0
, the Poincaré inequality shows

\|\Phi_{b_{i}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},\qquad\|\Phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

This implies that

\begin{split}&\Big|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}b_{i})\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x\Big|\\
&\lesssim\int_{\Omega}|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}|[e^{\lambda t}(a+2c)+\Theta_{i}((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f)]\mathrm{d}x+[\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]\int_{\Omega}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu^{1/4}e^{\lambda t}|f|\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&\qquad+\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu^{1/2}e^{\lambda t}|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\lambda\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}b_{i}\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&\qquad+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|\mu^{1/2}\Phi_{b_{i}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\\
&\Big|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(e^{\lambda t}c)\Phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x\Big|\\
&\lesssim\int_{\Omega}|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{c}||e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}+\Lambda((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f)|\mathrm{d}x+[\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{f}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]\int_{\Omega}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu^{1/4}e^{\lambda t}|f|\Phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&\qquad+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})|\mu^{1/2}\Phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v+\lambda\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\lambda t}c\Phi_{c}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.\end{split}

Combining with (
8.12
), we derive that

\sum_{i=1}^{3}\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{b_{i}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{c}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.

Therefore, we control the contribution of
\psi_{b_{i}},\psi_{c}
in
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
by

(8.13)

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{T}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{3}|e^{\lambda t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\partial_{t}\psi_{b_{i}}|+|e^{\lambda t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f\partial_{t}\psi_{c}|\Big)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}t\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}[\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}]\\
&\lesssim o(1)\|\mathbf{P}e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Collecting (
8.11
) and (
8.13
), and using the test function
\psi
in (
8.7
), we control
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
by

\begin{split}|\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}|&\lesssim\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+o(1)\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+[o(1)+\delta\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\delta\lambda]\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{P}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Since
\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\ll 1
from Theorem
1
and the inflow condition
1.14
, there exists
0<\lambda\ll 1
such that

(8.14)

\lambda+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll 1.

Collecting the estimates on
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{1}
–
\eqref{weak_formulation_dyna}_{11}
, together with the smallness condition (
8.14
), and absorbing the small terms into the left-hand side, we obtain

\displaystyle\delta\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\delta\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\delta_{1}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\delta_{1}\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{P}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2},

where the constant
0<\delta_{1}\ll 1
.
We now choose
\delta_{1}<\frac{\delta}{5}\ll 1
, which yields

\begin{split}&\frac{\delta}{2}\|e^{\lambda t}a\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\lambda t}\mathbf{b}\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\lambda t}c\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{2}\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Therefore, we conclude the lemma.
∎

Combining the energy estimate in Lemma
25
and the macroscopic estimate in Lemma
26
, we obtain the following
L^{2}
estimate.

Proposition 18
.

Let
(f,\phi_{f})
be a solution to (
1.17
) on
[0,T]
.
There exists
0<\lambda\ll 1
such that the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda T}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}.

Proof.

By Theorem
1
and the inflow condition (
1.14
), there exists a constant
0<\varepsilon<1
such that

\lambda+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\ll\varepsilon.

Multiplying (
8.5
) by
\varepsilon
and adding the result to (
8.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\lambda T}f(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda T}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(T)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+|e^{\lambda t}f|_{L^{2}_{T,\gamma_{+}}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|e^{\lambda t}\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}-\phi_{h})(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1/2}e^{\lambda t}\Gamma(f,f)\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\|e^{\lambda t/2}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+[\lambda+\|wh\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}]\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{2}_{T,x,\nu}}^{2}.

Absorbing the last term on the right-hand side into the left-hand side yields the proposition.
∎

Similar to Proposition
6
, we apply the
L^{2}
–
L^{\infty}
bootstrap argument together with the a priori
L^{2}
estimate in Proposition
18
to derive the following a priori
L^{\infty}
estimate.

Proposition 19
.

Let
(f,\phi_{f})
be a solution to (
1.17
) on
[0,T]
. Suppose
\|wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}<\infty
, then we have

(8.15)

\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}\lesssim\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Consequently, if
\|wf(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
is continuous in
t
and the assumption
\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\delta_{2}\ll 1
in Theorem
2
holds, then there exists a constant
C_{2}>1
such that

(8.16)

\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}<C_{2}\delta_{2}\ll 1.

Proof.

The proof of (
8.15
) is similar to the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate in Proposition
6
; see also the corresponding
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate in
[
3
]
. For conciseness, we omit the details.

For (
8.16
), we apply Proposition
18
to the term
\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x,v}}
in (
8.15
), which implies

\begin{split}\|e^{\lambda t}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x,v}}&\lesssim\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nu^{-1}e^{-\lambda t}w\Gamma(e^{\lambda t}f,e^{\lambda t}f)\|_{L^{2}_{T}L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\|e^{-\lambda t/2}e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{2}_{T}L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}^{2}+\|e^{\lambda t}wf\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x,v}}^{3/2}.\end{split}

Then (
8.16
) follows from a standard continuity argument under the smallness assumption on
\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
.
∎

8.2.
A priori uniform-in-time regularity estimate

In this section, we establish the a priori uniform-in-time regularity estimates for the dynamical problem (
1.17
) in Proposition
20
.
The argument is analogous to the a priori regularity estimates for the stationary problem developed in Sections
4
and
5
.

Due to the lack of control on
\nabla^{2}_{v}h
(see Remark
5
for further details), instead of considering
F=e^{-\phi_{E}}\mu+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}(h+f)
and the corresponding equation (
1.17
) for
f
, we rewrite the solution
F(t,x,v)
as

F(t,x,v)=e^{-\phi_{E}}\mu+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}\mathfrak{f}\ \text{ with }\ \mathfrak{f}=h+f.

The equations for
\mathfrak{f}
are given by

(8.17)

\begin{cases}&\partial_{t}\mathfrak{f}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{f}-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}}{2}\mathfrak{f}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\mathcal{L}\mathfrak{f}=-(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}})e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma(\mathfrak{f},\mathfrak{f}),\\
&\mathfrak{f}(x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=f_{b},\quad\mathfrak{f}(0,x,v)=\mathfrak{f}_{0}(x,v)=\frac{F(0,x,v)-e^{-\phi_{E}}\mu}{e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}},\\
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}=-\Delta_{x}\phi_{f}=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathfrak{f}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v\text{ in }\Omega,\quad\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\\
&-\partial_{n}\phi_{E}>C_{E}>0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

Note that the self-consistent fields in (
1.17
) and (
8.17
) are equal, namely,
\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}=\phi_{f}
.
Therefore, the potential field in (
8.17
) satisfies

E_{\mathfrak{f}}(t,x):=-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}+\phi_{E})=-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{f}+\phi_{E}).

Following the kinetic weight in (
1.18
), we continue to use the notation
\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)
for the dynamical kinetic weight associated with (
8.17
).
Moreover, we impose the following a priori assumption:

(8.18)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}=\|w(h+f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\ll 1,

(8.19)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\ll 1.

Under these assumptions,
E_{\mathfrak{f}}(t,x)
satisfies

E_{\mathfrak{f}}(t,x)\cdot n(\tilde{x})>\frac{C_{E}}{2}\ \text{ for }x\in\Omega_{\delta}.

Here, the first assumption (
8.18
) follows from Proposition
19
and Theorem
1
.
The second assumption (
8.19
) will be justified by Lemma
19
together with a continuity argument (see Section
8.4
for further details).

Proposition 20
.

Let
(\mathfrak{f},\phi_{\mathfrak{f}})
be a solution to (
8.17
).
Assume the a priori assumptions (
8.18
) and (
8.19
). Let
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\ll 1
be the constants defined in Theorem
1
and Theorem
2
. If the initial condition satisfies

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}\ll 1,

then for any
t>0
, there exists a time-independent constant
C_{4}>1
such that

(8.20)

\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{4}[\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}].

Proof.

For simplicity, we write
(X(s),V(s)):=(X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))
throughout the proof.
The proof of (
8.20
) is similar to the
W^{1,p}
and
C^{1}
estimates in Sections
4
and
5
.
In what follows, we focus on the key steps and the main differences from the previous arguments.

Analogous to (
3.8
) in the proof of Proposition
6
, we define the damping factor
\tilde{\nu}(t,x,v)
for (
8.17
) by

\tilde{\nu}(t,x,v):=e^{-\phi_{E}(x)}\nu(v)+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(t,x)}{2}.

From the assumptions (
8.18
) and (
8.19
), there exists a constant
T_{0}>1
such that

(8.21)

\|\nabla_{x}(\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}+\phi_{E})\|_{C^{1}_{t,x}}T_{0}\ll 1.

Moreover, Lemma
10
implies that

\tilde{\nu}(t,x,v)>\frac{\nu(v)}{2}>\frac{\nu_{0}}{2}\ \text{ for }0\leq t\leq T_{0}.

Analogously to (
4.5
), using (
4.4
) and Lemma
19
, we derive that for
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}\leq s\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

(8.22)

\begin{split}&|\nabla_{x}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))|\\
&\lesssim|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))||\nabla_{x}X(s)||\nu(V(s))|+|\nabla_{v}\nu(V(s))||\nabla_{x}V(s)|\\
&\qquad+|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))||\nabla_{x}X(s)||V(s)|+|\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))||\nabla_{x}V(s)|\\
&\lesssim e^{o(1)(t-s)}\nu(v)[o(1)(\|\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{f}\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}})+\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+1].\end{split}

We now claim that it suffices to prove that for
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

(8.23)

\begin{split}&\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big]\\
&\qquad+\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T_{0},x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

Assuming (
8.23
), by taking
t=T_{0}
and choosing
T_{0}>1
sufficiently large, we obtain

(8.24)

\begin{split}&\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(T_{0})\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\leq e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}\Big[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big]\\
&\qquad+C\Big[\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T_{0},x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big].\end{split}

From the assumption (
8.18
), we define

C^{\prime}:=C\Big[\sup_{t}\|w\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big]<\infty.

Iterating (
8.24
), we obtain for any
n\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
,

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}((n-1)T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}((n-1)T_{0})\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}((n-1)T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]+C^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{2\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}((n-2)T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}((n-2)T_{0})\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}((n-2)T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]+C^{\prime}[1+e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}]

\displaystyle\leq\ldots\leq e^{-\frac{n\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]+C^{\prime}\sum_{j=0}^{n}e^{-\frac{j\nu_{0}T_{0}}{8}}.

Hence, for every
n\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
,

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}(nT_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{t}\|w\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

where the implicit constant in the inequality is independent of
n
.
Furthermore, for any
n\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
and
(n-1)T_{0}\leq t\leq nT_{0}
, we apply (
8.23
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{t}\|w\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}},

where the implicit constant in the inequality is independent of
n
and
t
.
Applying Proposition
19
and Theorem
1
, we conclude the proposition for some constant
C_{4}>1
.

We now prove the claim (
8.23
).
Let
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
and define
t^{1}(t,x,v):=t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)
.
First, we show that for any
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

(8.25)

\begin{split}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}&\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+o(1)[\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{x,v}}]\\
&\qquad+\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.\end{split}

Applying the method of characteristics to (
8.17
), we obtain that for any
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

(8.26)

\begin{split}\mathfrak{f}(t,x,v)&=\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\\
&\qquad+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}\geq t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))\\
&\qquad+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s\\
&\qquad+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(\mathfrak{f},\mathfrak{f})(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s\\
&\qquad+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

Taking the derivative of (
8.26
) and multiplying both sides by
w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)
, we further obtain for any
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

\displaystyle w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t,x,v)

(8.27)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))[\partial_{x,v}X(0)\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{x,v}V(0)\cdot\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))]

(8.28)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\int^{t}_{0}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))

(8.29)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\int_{t^{1}}^{t}\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(8.30)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}\tilde{\nu}(t^{1},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

(8.31)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})[\partial_{x,v}x_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})+\partial_{x,v}v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})]

(8.32)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}[\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)]\mathrm{d}s

(8.33)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(8.34)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}u\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)e^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s

(8.35)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})}\mathbf{k}(v_{\mathbf{b}},u)f_{b}(x_{\mathbf{b}},u)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))]\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}

(8.36)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{d}ue^{-\phi_{E}(X(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\mathbf{k}(V(s),u)\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)\mathrm{d}s

(8.37)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})/2}\Gamma(f_{b},f_{b})(x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}

(8.38)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))]\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(\mathfrak{f},\mathfrak{f})(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

(8.39)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\partial_{x,v}[\Gamma(\mathfrak{f},\mathfrak{f})(s,X(s),V(s))]\mathrm{d}s

(8.40)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)}{2}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\Gamma(\mathfrak{f},\mathfrak{f})(s,X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s

(8.41)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\partial_{x,v}[\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))]e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(8.42)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\partial_{x,v}t_{\mathbf{b}}e^{-\int^{t}_{t^{1}}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v_{\mathbf{b}})v_{\mathbf{b}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(t^{1},x_{\mathbf{b}})e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(x_{\mathbf{b}})}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int_{s}^{t}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\int_{s}^{t}\mathrm{d}\tau\partial_{x,v}[\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))]

(8.43)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

(8.44)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))

(8.45)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\partial_{x,v}V(s)\cdot V(s)e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int^{t}_{\max\{t^{1},0\}}e^{-\int^{t}_{s}\tilde{\nu}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(s))V(s)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi_{\mathfrak{f}}(s,X(s))

(8.46)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi_{E}(X(s))\partial_{x,v}X(s)}{2}e^{-\frac{\phi_{E}(X(s))}{2}}\sqrt{\mu(V(s))}\mathrm{d}s.

We note that this piecewise derivative formula gives a weak derivative of (
8.26
) due to the compatibility condition
f_{0}|_{\gamma_{-}}=0
and
\mathfrak{f}(0,x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=h|_{\gamma_{-}}=f_{b}
; see
[
26
,
5
]
.

Similar to (
4.28
) in Proposition
7
, we split the terms (
8.27
)-(
8.46
) into four parts, denoted by
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{D}
, as follows:

(8.47)

\begin{split}\mathcal{A}(t,x,v)&=\eqref{hchara:nabla_nu_1}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_nu_2}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_K}+\eqref{hchara:K_nabla_phi_E}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_nu_K}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_nu_gamma}+\eqref{hchara:gamma_nabla_phi_E}\\
&\qquad+\eqref{hchara:nabla_E}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_nu_phi}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_V_phi}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_mu_phi}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_phi_E_phi},\\
\mathcal{B}(t,x,v)&=\eqref{hchara:nabla_0}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_gamma},\\
\mathcal{C}(t,x,v)&=\eqref{hchara:nabla_tb}+\eqref{hchara:bdr}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_int}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_tb_gamma}+\eqref{hchara:nabla_tb_phi},\\
\mathcal{D}(t,x,v)&=\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}.\end{split}

For
\mathcal{A}(t,x,v)
, we follow (
4.30
) by replacing
h(X(0),V(0))
with
\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))
and conclude that

\|\mathcal{A}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim o(1)(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{f}\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}})+\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+\|w\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}

For
\mathcal{B}(t,x,v)
, we follow (
4.35
) by replacing
h(X(0),V(0))
with
\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))
and
o(1)
with
e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}
, and conclude that

\|\mathcal{B}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4}}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+o(1)\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{x,v}}.

For
\mathcal{C}(t,x,v)
, we follow (
4.37
) and conclude that

\|\mathcal{C}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

For
\mathcal{D}(t,x,v)
, similarly to the proof of Proposition
7
, we decompose the term
\partial_{x,v}[\mathfrak{f}(s,X(s),u)]
in (
8.32
) into four parts as in (
8.47
). Consequently, (
8.32
) can be expanded as

\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}=\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{A}}+\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{B}}+\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{C}}+\eqref{hchara:K_nabla}_{\mathcal{D}}.

The estimates for most of the resulting terms follow from (
4.56
). The only difference arises from the contribution (
8.27
) associated with the initial data, denote by
\eqref{hchara:nabla_K}_{\eqref{hchara:nabla_0}}
. Similar to (
4.42
), we compute

\displaystyle\|\eqref{hchara:nabla_K}_{\mathcal{B}}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big\|\int^{t}_{\max\{t-t_{\mathbf{b}},0\}}\mathrm{d}se^{-\frac{\nu(v)(t-s)}{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{k}(v,u)\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)}e^{-\frac{\nu(u)s}{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times w_{\tilde{\theta}}(u)[|\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))|+|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0;s,x,u),V(0;s,x,u))|]\Big\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}.

Therefore, we conclude that

\|\mathcal{D}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|w\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+o(1)\big(\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|\alpha_{f}\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\big).

Combining the above estimates for
\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B},\mathcal{C}
, and
\mathcal{D}
, we conclude (
8.25
).

Second, we estimate
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
and
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
.
Compared to Proposition
8
and Proposition
9
, the only difference comes from the contribution (
8.27
) associated with the initial data.

For the contribution of (
8.27
) to
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
, we apply (
4.4
) together with (
8.21
) to obtain that for any
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

\displaystyle\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)\Big|e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))[\partial_{x}X(0)\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{x}V(0)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{C_{0}}\alpha_{f}(0,X(0),V(0))e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}|w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))|\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}\|\alpha_{f}(0,x,v)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the contribution of (
8.27
) to
\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
, we obtain that for any
0\leq t\leq T_{0}
,

\displaystyle\Big|e^{-\int^{t}_{0}\tilde{\nu}(X(s),V(s))\mathrm{d}s}\frac{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)}{w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))}w_{\tilde{\theta}}(V(0))[\partial_{v}X(0)\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{v}V(0)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\alpha_{f}(t,x,v)\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))+\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(X(0),V(0))|e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\nu_{0}t/4}[\|\alpha_{f}(0,x,v)w_{\tilde{\theta}}(v)\partial_{x}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}].

Combining the above estimates and (
8.25
), we derive that

\displaystyle\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{f}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{f}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{t}\|w\mathfrak{f}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|wf_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}+|w\partial_{\mathbf{x}_{p},v}f_{b}|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}.

Therefore, we conclude the claim (
8.23
).
∎

8.3.
Local well-posedness

In this section, we establish the local well-posedness for the dynamical problem (
1.16
) in Proposition
21
.
Similar to Section
8.2
, due to the lack of control on
\nabla_{v}^{2}h
, we avoid the decomposition

F=F_{s}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\sqrt{\mu}f
and the corresponding equation (
1.17
) for
f
. However, unlike Section
8.2
, we now rewrite
F(t,x,v)
as follows to adapt to the local well-posedness argument.

(8.48)

F(t,x,v)=\sqrt{\mu}g\ \text{ with }\ g=\frac{F_{s}}{\sqrt{\mu}}+e^{-\phi_{E}/2}f.

The equations for
g
are given by

(8.49)

\begin{cases}&\partial_{t}g+v\cdot\nabla_{x}g-\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}g+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi_{g}+\phi_{E})}{2}g+\nu(\sqrt{\mu}g)g=e^{-\phi_{E}/2}\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(g,g),\\
&g(t,x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}},\quad g(0,x,v)=g_{0}(x,v)=\frac{F_{0}(x,v)}{\sqrt{\mu}},\\
&-\Delta_{x}\phi_{g}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v-e^{-\phi_{E}}\text{ in }\Omega,\quad\phi_{g}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\\
&-\partial_{n}\phi_{E}>C_{E}>0\text{ on }\partial\Omega,\end{cases}

where
\nu(\sqrt{\mu}g):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(v-u)\cdot\omega|\sqrt{\mu(u)}g(u)\mathrm{d}\omega\mathrm{d}u
denotes the collision frequency corresponding to the distribution
\sqrt{\mu}g
.
By direct computation and the definition of the kinetic weight in (
1.18
), it follows that

\phi_{g}=\phi_{f},\qquad\alpha_{g}(t,x,v)=\alpha_{f}(t,x,v).

Proposition 21
.

Assume that all assumptions in Theorem
1
and Theorem
2
hold.
There exist constants
\delta_{3}\ll 1
and
C_{5}>0
such that if the initial data
F_{0}=F_{s}+e^{-\phi_{E}}\sqrt{\mu}f_{0}=\sqrt{\mu}g_{0}\geq 0
satisfies the compatibility condition
g_{0}(x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}}
and

(8.50)

\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\delta_{3},\qquad\|wg_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{v}g_{0}\|_{L^{3}_{x,v}}<C_{5},

then there exists a unique non-negative solution
F=\sqrt{\mu}g(t)\geq 0
to (
1.16
) on
[0,T^{\prime}]
, where
T^{\prime}=T^{\prime}(\delta_{3})\ll 1
.
Moreover, there exists a constant
C_{6}>1
such that for any
t\in[0,T^{\prime}]
,

(8.51)

\|wg(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}g(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<C_{6}C_{5}.

Furthermore,
\|wg(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\nabla_{v}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\partial_{x,v}g(t)\|_{L^{p}_{x,v}},\|w_{\tilde{\theta}}\alpha_{g}\partial_{x,v}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
are continuous on
[0,T^{\prime}]
.

Proof.

The proof is analogous to Theorem 4 in
[
2
]
. We sketch the proof and highlight the main differences.

We construct a solution to (
8.49
) via the following iterative sequence: for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

(8.52)

\begin{cases}&\Big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\nabla_{x}(\phi^{m}+\phi_{E})\cdot\nabla_{v}+\frac{v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\phi^{m}+\phi_{E})}{2}+\nu(\sqrt{\mu}g^{m})\Big)g^{m+1}=\Gamma_{\text{gain}}(g^{m},g^{m}),\\
&g^{m+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}=\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}},\quad g^{m+1}(0,x,v)=g_{0}(x,v),\\
&-\Delta_{x}\phi^{m}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g^{m}\sqrt{\mu}\mathrm{d}v-e^{-\phi_{E}}\text{ in }\Omega,\quad\phi^{m}=0\text{ on }\partial\Omega.\end{cases}

where the initial setting
g^{0}=\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}}
.

Compared to
[
2
]
, we note the following two main differences.
One is that the boundary condition
g^{m+1}|_{\gamma_{-}}=\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}}
in (
8.52
) is of inflow type, so characteristics interact with the boundary at most once, which is simpler than the diffuse reflection case in
[
2
]
.
Another is that the Poisson equation in (
8.52
) is equipped with a Dirichlet boundary condition
\phi^{m}|_{\partial\Omega}=0
, rather than a Neumann boundary condition.
Thus, to apply the framework of
[
2
]
, we must ensure the favorable sign condition: for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

(8.53)

-\partial_{n}(\phi^{m}+\phi_{E})>\frac{C_{E}}{2}\text{ on }\partial\Omega.

First, we establish a uniform-in-
m

L^{\infty}_{x,v}
estimate for
\{g^{m}\}^{\infty}_{m=1}
, which is not affected by the sign condition (
8.53
).
Using a standard characteristic argument (simpler than in
[
2
]
due to the inflow boundary), there exists
C>1
and
T^{\prime}=T^{\prime}(\|wg_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\ll 1
such that

\sup_{m}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{\prime}}\|e^{(\theta-t)|v|^{2}}g^{m}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<C\Big[\|e^{\theta|v|^{2}}g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\Big|w\frac{F_{b}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\Big|_{L^{\infty}_{\partial\Omega,v}}\Big].

By (
8.48
),
T^{\prime}
depends only on
\|wf_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\delta_{3}
, i.e.,
